Abstract: Message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) iteratively propagate and aggregate local neighborhood information followed by global readout to learn graph representations. However, their discriminative power is upper-bounded by the Weisfeiler--Lehman (1-WL) graph isomorphism test. This prevents GNNs from distinguishing certain non-isomorphic graphs with identical local neighborhood structures, often leading to similar graph representations. Unified Topological Signatures (UTS) capture compact, multi-scale representation of global graph topology derived from persistent homology. We introduce two complementary UTS signatures: Graph_UTS- a static signature of the input graph topology, and Embedding_UTS- a dynamic signature of the evolving embedding topology. They encode structural information inaccessible to 1-WL-based message-passing GNNs, yet their capabilities are explored solely for post-hoc embedding-space analysis. We integrate UTS into GNN training across three architectural interventions: (i) UTS-Aug: augmenting with standard readout feature that encodes graph's true topology; (ii) UTS-Reg: topological regularizer that constrains representation collapse; (iii) UTS-Pool: topology-guided pooling that retains structurally critical nodes. We further leverage UTS as a layer-wise diagnostic to quantify oversmoothing during GNN training. Theoretically, we show that integrating UTS into GNN optimization strictly extends GNN expressivity beyond the 1-WL hierarchy. Experiments on three graph classification benchmarks show consistent benefits: Graph-UTS, Dual-UTS, and UTS-Pool improve accuracy across all three datasets, Embedding-UTS provides smaller but similarly consistent gains, and UTS-Reg's benefit varies across graph domains. Accuracy improves by up to 5.8% with Graph-UTS augmentation, by up to 1.9% with UTS-Reg, and achieves comparable performance to TOGL with UTS-Pool.

</details>


### [248] [High-Fidelity Digital Twin Data Models by Randomized Dynamic Mode Decomposition and Deep Learning with Applications in Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17101)
*Diana A. Bistrian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合随机动态模态分解与深度学习的人工智能框架，以非侵入方式从数值代码输出中构建高保真数字孪生数据模型（DTM），并在三个复杂度递增的激波现象数值模拟中验证其精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统降阶建模通常需要将控制方程进行Galerkin投影，侵入性较强，且面对随时间演化、复杂度高的动力学问题时，探索成本高、硬件与CPU时间开销大。作者希望构建一种低复杂度但能高精度复现原始过程行为的数字孪生数据模型。

Method: 定义数字孪生数据模型（DTM）为一种低复杂度、能够镜像原始过程行为的模型；采用非侵入式技术（不要求将控制方程Galerkin投影到降阶模态基上），融合随机动态模态分解（randomized dynamic mode decomposition）与深度学习人工智能，形成新的DTM构建框架。

Result: 所构建的DTM输出与原始源数据保持一致，同时具有降低复杂度的优势；在三个复杂度递增的激波现象数值模拟中，作者从数值精度和计算效率两方面对新型数字孪生数据模型进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: 结合随机动态模态分解与深度学习的非侵入式框架能够有效生成高保真数字孪生数据模型，在保持与原始数据一致性的同时降低复杂性，适用于复杂动力学场景下的高效建模与仿真。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is the identification of high-fidelity digital twin data models from numerical code outputs by non-intrusive techniques (i.e., not requiring Galerkin projection of the governing equations onto the reduced modes basis). In this paper the author defines the concept of the digital twin data model (DTM) as a model of reduced complexity that has the main feature of mirroring the original process behavior. The significant advantage of a DTM is to reproduce the dynamics with high accuracy and reduced costs in CPU time and hardware for settings difficult to explore because of the complexity of the dynamics over time. This paper introduces a new framework for creating efficient digital twin data models by combining two state-of-the-art tools: randomized dynamic mode decomposition and deep learning artificial intelligence. It is shown that the outputs are consistent with the original source data with the advantage of reduced complexity. The DTMs are investigated in the numerical simulation of three shock wave phenomena with increasing complexity. The author performs a thorough assessment of the performance of the new digital twin data models in terms of numerical accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [249] [Neural Field Ensembles for Aerodynamic Surface Prediction: Winning Solution to the ONERA CRM Wall Distribution 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17160)
*Lionel Salesses,Caroline Sainvitu,Tariq Benamara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件神经场方法，通过映射空间坐标、表面法向与工况预测NASA CRM构型的气动壁面量（压力与摩擦系数分布），在ONERA挑战赛中夺冠，测试得分8.81，优于基线8.64，且可训练参数少约三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD仿真代价高昂，机器学习代理模型是有前景的替代方案，但面向真实复杂飞机外形（复杂几何、多流态、训练数据有限）构建高精度代理模型仍具挑战。本文旨在解决ONERA CRM壁面分布回归挑战赛的任务，即预测NASA通用研究模型（CRM）翼身-挂架-短舱构型在不同工况下的压力与摩擦系数分布。

Method: 将问题建模为条件神经场，把空间坐标、表面法向和工况映射到气动壁面量。逐步引入傅里叶特征编码、与挑战赛指标一致的相对平方误差目标、集成学习以及k折交叉验证，以提升精度并充分利用有限数据；同时通过系统的消融实验记录模型设计决策的演进，并讨论若干被放弃的备选方案。

Result: 在隐藏的竞赛测试集上，方法整体得分为8.81，优于组织方提供的最强基线（8.64），而所需可训练参数约少三个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明，精心设计的基于坐标的神经场是复杂几何、有限数据条件下高效且鲁棒的气动代理建模框架。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogate models offer a promising alternative to high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations for aerodynamic analysis and design. However, constructing accurate surrogates for realistic aircraft configurations remain challenging due to complex geometries, multiple flow regimes, and limited training data. This work presents the methodology that achieved first place in the ONERA CRM Wall Distribution Regression Challenge, which focuses on predicting pressure and skin-friction coefficient distributions over the NASA Common Research Model wing-body-pylon-nacelle configuration under different operating conditions. The proposed approach formulates the problem as a conditional neural field mapping spatial coordinates, surface normals, and operating conditions to aerodynamic wall quantities. Fourier feature encoding, a relative squared error objective aligned with the challenge metric, ensemble learning, and $k$-fold cross-validation are progressively introduced to improve prediction accuracy and exploit the limited training data. Beyond presenting the final methodology, the paper documents the successive model design choices that led to the winning solution through a comprehensive ablation study and discusses several alternative approaches that were investigated but ultimately discarded. On the hidden competition test set, the proposed methodology achieves an overall score of 8.81, outperforming the strongest organizer-provided baseline, which achieved a score of 8.64, while requiring approximately three orders of magnitude fewer trainable parameters. These results illustrate that carefully designed coordinate-based neural fields constitute an efficient and robust framework for aerodynamic surrogate modeling on complex geometries under limited-data conditions.

</details>


### [250] [A unified framework for global and local interpretability using adaptive derivative-ordered random explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17171)
*Lemen Chao,Ming Lei,Anran Fanga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出ADORE方法，利用一阶与二阶导数在统一框架内建模非线性特征交互并捕捉特征-样本交互，结合随机SVD与动态稀疏检测提升计算效率，在表格、文本、图像三类模态上优于LIME和SHAP，并开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗、金融等高风险领域对复杂机器学习模型的可解释性要求极高。现有事后可解释性方法存在分析流程碎片化、难以建模非线性特征交互、计算效率低、过度依赖特定模型架构等固有局限，亟需一种统一且高效的解释方案。

Method: 提出自适应导数阶随机解释方法ADORE，利用一阶和二阶导数应对非线性模型复杂度，在统一分析框架中捕捉特征-样本交互；将全局特征重要性与局部样本贡献相结合，通过同时刻画影响幅度与方向来量化特征作用，并识别影响模型决策的关键样本；借助随机奇异值分解与动态稀疏检测实现计算效率，可扩展至大规模高维数据集。

Result: 在表格、文本、图像三种数据模态上的实验表明，ADORE在处理复杂交互和计算效率方面优于LIME、SHAP等现有方法，并提供更详细且可靠的解释。

Conclusion: ADORE在统一框架下兼顾了非线性交互建模、特征-样本交互捕捉与计算效率，具有跨模态的通用性与可扩展性；作者已将其开源为Python包并托管于GitHub，便于研究者与从业者复现和应用于具体任务、模型与数据集。

Abstract: The interpretability of complex machine learning models is of paramount importance, especially in real-world high-stakes domains such as healthcare and finance. However, existing post-hoc interpretability methods suffer from inherent limitations: fragmented analytical processes, inadequate capacity to model nonlinear feature interactions, computational inefficiencies, and over-reliance on specific model architectures. To address these challenges, this paper provides a novel method - Adaptive Derivative-Ordered Random Explanation (ADORE) - that leverages first- and second-order derivatives to accommodate nonlinear model complexities, while enabling effective capture of feature-sample interactions within a unified analytical framework. ADORE integrates global feature importance with local sample contributions, precisely quantifying feature impact by capturing both magnitude and direction, and identifying critical samples influencing model decisions. Furthermore, it achieves computational efficiency through randomized singular value decomposition (SVD) and dynamic sparsity detection, making it scalable to large, high-dimensional datasets. Experiments across three data modalities - tabular, text, and image - demonstrate that ADORE outperforms existing methods such as LIME and SHAP in handling complex interactions and computational efficiency, while providing detailed and reliable explanations. To facilitate adoption and reproducibility, ADORE has been released as an open-source Python package, hosted on GitHub, enabling researchers and practitioners to readily adapt and apply our approach to their specific tasks, models, and datasets.

</details>


### [251] [IRENE: A Convolutional GRU Ensemble Model for Radar Precipitation Nowcasting over Italy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17175)
*Alessandro Camilletti,Gabriele Franch,Elena Tomasi,Marco Cristoforetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRENE 是一套面向意大利域、1 km/5 min 分辨率的深度学习概率降水临近预报模型，采用多尺度 ConvGRU 编码器-预报器架构，并衍生出 GAN 与谱约束两个变体，在 CRPS 与集合校准上优于 STEPS 和 DGMR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时降水临近预报需要高分辨率、概率化且空间锐利的预测。现有方法（随机外推 STEPS、深度学习 DGMR）在高分辨率概率技巧与空间细节保持方面仍有不足，尤其深度学习模型存在小尺度方差随预报时效逐渐衰减的问题。本文旨在为意大利域构建一套性能更优的概率临近预报系统，并探索改善空间锐度与谱特性的训练策略。

Method: 模型采用基于多尺度卷积门控循环单元（ConvGRU）的编码器-预报器架构，在意大利民防部（DPC）全国雷达拼图上训练，空间分辨率 1 km、时间分辨率 5 min。训练中使用重要性采样聚焦降水相关事件，以近乎公平的连续分级概率评分（afCRPS）作为主要概率损失。提出两种额外配置：对抗（GAN）变体 IRENE-GAN 用于提升预报空间锐度；谱约束变体 IRENE-GAN-RAPSD 在对抗目标上加入对径向平均功率谱密度（RAPSD）的显式惩罚。三个配置与 STEPS、预训练 DGMR 进行对比评估。

Result: 所有 IRENE 配置在所有预报时效上的 CRPS 均低于 STEPS 和 DGMR，且秩直方图更接近均匀分布，说明概率技巧更好、集合校准更优。在集合平均 MAE 上，优势仅局限于前 90 分钟，之后强阻尼的 DGMR 场以及（程度较轻的）STEPS 变得具有竞争力。谱分析显示，对抗训练消除了 IRENE 原有的小尺度方差逐步损失，但代价是长预报时效下细尺度功率过剩，谱惩罚只能部分抑制该现象。

Conclusion: IRENE 在高分辨率概率降水临近预报中展现出优于 STEPS 与 DGMR 的概率技巧和集合校准，尤其在 CRPS 指标上全面领先。对抗训练能有效恢复小尺度结构，但会引入长时效的细尺度功率过剩，谱约束仅能部分缓解。整体而言，该工作验证了多尺度 ConvGRU 结合 afCRPS 与重要性采样在区域临近预报中的有效性，并揭示了锐度与谱保真度之间的权衡。

Abstract: We present IRENE (Italian Radar Ensemble Nowcasting Experiment), a deep learning model for probabilistic short-range precipitation nowcasting over the Italian domain at \SI{1}{km} spatial and 5 min temporal resolution. IRENE adopts an encoder--forecaster architecture built on multi-scale Convolutional Gated Recurrent Units (ConvGRUs), trained on the national radar composite produced by the Italian Civil Protection Department (DPC). An importance-sampling scheme focuses training on precipitation-relevant events, while the almost-fair Continuous Ranked Probability Score (afCRPS) is adopted as the primary probabilistic loss function. Two additional training configurations are proposed: an adversarial (GAN) variant, IRENE-GAN, designed to improve the spatial sharpness of the generated forecasts, and a spectrally constrained variant, IRENE-GAN-RAPSD, in which the adversarial objective is complemented by an explicit penalty on the radially averaged power spectral density. The three configurations are evaluated against the stochastic extrapolation method STEPS and the pre-trained deep learning model DGMR. All IRENE configurations attain a lower Continuous Ranked Probability Score than both benchmarks at every lead time and rank histograms closer to uniformity, indicating better probabilistic skill and ensemble calibration. In terms of ensemble-mean mean absolute error the advantage is confined to the first 90 min, beyond which the strongly damped DGMR fields and, to a lesser extent, STEPS become competitive. Spectral analysis shows that the adversarial training removes the progressive loss of small-scale variance exhibited by IRENE, at the cost of an excess of fine-scale power at long lead times that the spectral penalty only partially controls.

</details>


### [252] [LoopSpec: Pipelined Self-Speculative Decoding for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17184)
*SangLyul Cho,Langqing Cui,Sehoon Kim,Dongsu Han,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopSpec是一个针对循环Transformer（Looped Transformers）的无训练自推测解码框架，通过从早期循环状态提取草稿token、流水线重叠执行、并结合深层循环的选择性二次提案，实现最高6.83倍推理加速且无损解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer以紧凑参数量获得强性能，但因每个循环深度都要访问共享权重，解码延迟高于同等参数量标准Transformer。其循环中间状态天然可作草稿预测，适合自推测解码，无需额外草稿模型。

Method: 提出LoopSpec无训练自推测解码框架：从早期循环状态提取草稿token；采用流水线方式，让未来token的草稿生成与当前token的目标验证重叠；引入来自更深循环深度的选择性第二次提案，在不过多增加计算开销下提升草稿准确率；推导最优提案深度的闭式解。

Result: 在贪婪与采样两种模式下均保证无损解码；解析得到的最优提案深度与实测结果吻合；在推理与编码基准上，对多种循环Transformer实现最高6.83倍推理加速。

Conclusion: LoopSpec验证了自推测解码与循环Transformer结构的良好契合，可在无训练、无损前提下显著提升其解码效率，为循环架构的高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Looped Transformers achieve strong performance with compact parameter sizes by repeatedly applying a shared stack of Transformer blocks across recurrent depths. However, they incur higher decoding latency than standard Transformer models of comparable parameter size because shared weights are accessed at every recurrent depth. To improve decoding efficiency, self-speculative decoding is particularly well suited to Looped Transformers, as their intermediate recurrent states can directly provide draft predictions without an auxiliary draft model. We therefore propose LoopSpec, a training-free self-speculative decoding framework tailored for Looped Transformers. LoopSpec extracts draft tokens from early recurrent states and operates in a pipelined manner, overlapping draft generation of future tokens with target verification of the current token. To improve draft accuracy without excessive compute overhead, we introduce a selective second proposal from deeper recurrent depth while ensuring lossless decoding under both greedy and sampling regimes. Furthermore, we derive the optimal proposal depths in closed form and show the prediction matches measurement. Across reasoning and coding benchmarks, LoopSpec achieves up to 6.83$\times$ inference speedup across diverse Looped Transformers.

</details>


### [253] [MyoFlow: Anchor-Tied Rectified Flow for HD-sEMG Gesture Recognition Across Sessions and Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17194)
*Chenhao Wu,Dingjie Peng,Satoshi Funabashi,Satoshi Konishi,Wuqiang Yang,Hiroshi Onoda,Hironori Washizaki,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MyoFlow，首个用于HD-sEMG跨会话与跨被试识别的判别式流匹配框架，将分类重构为锚点约束的传输问题，实现零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HD-sEMG手势识别受电极重戴和生理变异导致的分布偏移影响，跨会话与跨被试精度下降；现有生成式HD-sEMG模型（如扩散模型）主要用于信号增强，预测仍需依赖独立分类器，学到的动态与决策规则脱节。

Method: MyoFlow是判别式流匹配框架：将分类重构为锚点约束传输（anchor-tied transport）。使用域条件化的整流流（rectified flow）将编码后的信号窗口移动到手势锚点，锚点既作为传输目标又定义最近锚点决策几何，从而无需独立分类头即可零样本预测。

Result: 在Hyser数据集上，跨会话与跨被试平均精度比最强扩散基线分别提升4.24%和6.37%；在CEMHSEY数据集上跨多天取得91.71%平均零样本精度和97.39%平均少样本精度。

Conclusion: 将生成式动态与决策规则统一于判别式流匹配，可有效缓解HD-sEMG的分布偏移，在跨会话、跨被试及零/少样本场景下均优于扩散基线。

Abstract: High-density surface electromyography (HD-sEMG) gesture recognition supports prosthetic control, assistive robotics, and rehabilitation, but electrode re-donning and physiological variability cause distribution shifts that degrade accuracy across sessions and subjects. Generative HD-sEMG models primarily synthesize signals for augmentation; although diffusion models enhance representation learning, prediction still relies on a separate classifier. To tie learned dynamics to the decision rule, we propose MyoFlow, the first discriminative flow-matching framework for HD-sEMG recognition across sessions and subjects. It recasts classification as anchor-tied transport: a domain-conditioned rectified flow moves encoded windows toward gesture anchors that serve as transport targets and define the nearest-anchor decision geometry, enabling zero-shot prediction without an independent head. On the Hyser dataset, MyoFlow improves mean cross-session and cross-subject accuracy over the strongest diffusion-based baseline by 4.24\% and 6.37\%, respectively, and achieves 91.71\% mean zero-shot accuracy and 97.39\% mean few-shot accuracy across multiple days on the CEMHSEY dataset.

</details>


### [254] [Memorisation bias in medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17223)
*Moritz A. Knolle,Martin J. Menten,Laurin Lux,Mélanie Roschewitz,Emma A. M. Stanley,Georgios Kaissis,Daniel Rueckert,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了"记忆偏差"（memorisation bias）现象：若模型训练时见过某患者的匿名历史数据，其对该患者未来新数据的预测会显著改变，跨模态、跨架构、跨数十年均存在，并在模拟前瞻部署中对回访患者的诊断准确性产生不对称影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学AI模型虽有潜力改善患者结局，但已知会无意记住训练集中的个体记录。以往研究多关注针对性隐私攻击，而记忆对临床部署的后果——即患者可能被一个训练时见过其历史数据的模型评估——仍理解不足。本文旨在刻画这一被忽视的临床风险。

Method: 作者通过对比"训练时见过该患者历史数据"与"未见过"的两个其他条件相同的模型，在患者未见的未来数据上进行预测比较，以识别记忆偏差。验证覆盖多种数据模态与模型架构，并跨越长时间跨度（部分案例达数十年）。此外开展模拟前瞻性部署实验，考察回访数据贡献者（returning contributors）在健康状况变化与未变化两种情形下的诊断敏感度与特异度表现。

Result: 记忆偏差在多种数据模态、模型架构及长时间跨度上普遍存在，某些情况下可持续到训练用历史记录之后数十年采集的未来记录。模拟前瞻部署显示其影响具有不对称性：当患者带着历史记录中不存在的新发疾病回访时，诊断敏感度较未训练于其历史数据的相同模型显著下降；而当健康状况未变时，敏感度与特异度均显著虚高。

Conclusion: 本文揭示医学AI中一个此前未被识别的风险：模型被部署到曾贡献训练数据的患者身上时会出现记忆偏差。这暴露了当前模型开发实践的缺陷——为保护隐私而设计的去标识化措施，反而使识别回访贡献者并将其排除在自身未来数据的AI辅助判读之外变得困难。缓解记忆风险可能需要改变现有的模型训练与部署流程。

Abstract: Medical AI models hold immense potential to improve patient outcomes, but they are also known to unintentionally memorise individual records from their training datasets. While such memorisation has been linked to targeted privacy attacks, its consequences for clinical deployment, where patients may be assessed by a model that saw their historical data during training, remain poorly understood. Here we show that predictions on a patient's unseen future data can change significantly if a model observed that same patient's anonymised historical data during training, a phenomenon we term "memorisation bias". We demonstrate that this bias exists across diverse data modalities and model architectures, and over prolonged time spans: in some cases, memorisation bias persists on future records acquired decades after the historical records used for training. Moreover, in simulated prospective deployment, memorisation bias has asymmetric effects on the diagnostic accuracy of returning data contributors. When a patient returned with a de novo condition absent from their historical records in the training dataset, diagnostic sensitivity decreased significantly compared to an otherwise identical model not trained on their historical data. Conversely, when their health state was unchanged, both sensitivity and specificity were significantly inflated. Our findings reveal a previously uncharacterised risk in medical AI that arises when a model is deployed on patients who contributed to its training data. This exposes a shortcoming of current model development practice: the de-identification measures designed to protect patients' privacy make it difficult to identify returning contributors and exclude them from the AI-assisted interpretation of their own future data. Mitigating memorisation risks may thus require changes to current model training and deployment protocols.

</details>


### [255] [Easy to Catch a Liar, Hard to Clear an Honest One: Language Models Diagnosing a Corrupted Reward Channel from a Verified Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17226)
*Arman Nik Khah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验冻结的大语言模型能否利用"关于报告者自身的额外数据"来区分"世界变化"与"报告者撒谎"，结果发现模型对撒谎者几乎总能识别，但会把诚实报告者大量误判为撒谎者，且该失败由与任务无关的表面特征驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习理论表明，当奖励报告突然改变时，仅凭报告本身无法区分"世界变了"还是"报告者坏了"，必须引入关于报告者的更丰富数据。作者想检验：如果直接把这类数据交给一个冻结的语言模型，它会不会正确使用。此前已注册了对关键误判率的预测（58% 场景预测 35%）。

Method: 构建一个两选项博弈：收益互换（世界变化）与撒谎报告者会产生逐字节完全相同的历史。随后加入一条经过验证的记录——对某一轮真实结果的独立核查，打印在该轮报告者说法旁边，理论上这一行即可定案。让来自两个家族、三种规模的大模型（含32B、70B/72B）用一个字母回答单一问题：报告者是诚实还是撒谎。

Result: 模型识别撒谎者近乎完美，70B级别在所有条件下都成立，32B仅在某一种措辞下失误。但对诚实报告者的判定差得多，且受不应相关的因素影响：在轮次、字母、措辞上取平均，72B在有变化时把诚实者误判为撒谎者58%，在毫无变化时也有38%；另一家族70B分别为48%和26%。这不是阅读能力问题，因为在"答案已印在提示中"的无变化情形下，同批模型得分0.96–1.00。驱动失败的表层特征因家族而异：Qwen受记录指涉哪一轮影响，Llama受哪个字母代表"诚实"影响；把记录加入已含答案的提示，反而让Llama更不易给出该答案。实测58%远高于事先注册的35%预测。

Conclusion: 冻结语言模型未能稳健地利用关于报告者的验证数据：它们擅长揪出撒谎者，却系统性地把诚实者误判为撒谎者，且该偏差由无关的表面特征（轮次指代、字母映射）决定，说明模型并非在按逻辑推理，而是受提示表层形式支配，失败程度超出作者预期。

Abstract: An agent that learns from rewards has to trust whatever reports those rewards. When the reports suddenly change, either the world changed or the reporter broke. From the reports alone these are indistinguishable, and reinforcement learning theory shows that no amount of further experience separates them. The prescribed escape is richer data about the reporter itself. We ask whether a frozen language model, handed exactly that data, uses it. We build a two-option game in which a payout swap and a lying reporter produce byte-identical histories. Then we add one verified record: an independent check of one round's real result, printed beside what the reporter said about that round. That single line settles the case. We ask three large models, from two families, to answer one question with one letter. Is the reporter honest or lying? They catch a lying reporter almost perfectly. At the 70B class that holds in every condition we tried; the 32B model slips in one wording. They clear an honest reporter far less often, and how often depends on things that should not matter. Averaged over rounds, letters, and wordings, a 72B model calls an honest reporter a liar 38% of the time when nothing has changed at all, and 58% of the time when the payouts moved. A 70B model from a second family calls an honest reporter a liar 26% and 48% of the time. The failure is not one of reading, because in the situation where nothing changed the same models score 0.96 to 1.00 with the answer printed in the prompt. Which surface feature drives it differs by family. For the Qwen models it is which round the record names, and for Llama it is which letter stands for "honest." Adding the record to a prompt that already states the answer makes Llama less likely to give that answer. We had registered a prediction for that 58% before the run: 35%. The failure is larger than we expected.

</details>


### [256] [Personalized Federated Learning through Global Knowledge Distillation and Local Head Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17284)
*Polycarpo Souza Neto,José Mairton Barros da Silva Júnior,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出pFedKDH，在联邦学习中仅聚合共享主干、保留客户端个性化头，并用重校准的全局头作为教师进行知识蒸馏，缓解统计异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的统计异质性导致单一全局分类器无法表征各客户端的标签分布，需要个性化方法。

Method: 只聚合共享主干网络，保留持久的客户端特定头；在本地训练时使用重校准的全局头作为教师进行知识蒸馏，引导本地优化。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR10和CIFAR100的类别狄利克雷划分下，多数设置取得最佳精度，最高比最弱基线高出37.67%，且多次重复的标准差较低。

Conclusion: 组件诊断与收敛结果支持持久头和蒸馏引导的本地优化在标签偏斜数据下的作用，pFedKDH有效缓解统计异质性。

Abstract: Statistical heterogeneity limits federated learning when a single global classifier cannot represent client-specific label distributions. In this work, we propose Personalized Federated Knowledge Distillation with Head Adaptation (pFedKDH), which aggregates only the shared backbone, keeps persistent client-specific heads, and uses a recalibrated global head as a teacher during local training. Across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR10, and CIFAR100 under class-wise Dirichlet partitions, pFedKDH obtains the best accuracy in most settings, with accuracy gaps up to 37.67\% over the weakest baseline and consistently low standard deviation across repetitions. Component-wise diagnostics and convergence results support the role of persistent heads and distillation-guided local optimization under label-skewed data.

</details>


### [257] [Same Flow, Different Paths: Variance Reduction in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17287)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化（随机梯度方差）视角研究流匹配中路径选择问题，发现在FM目标相同的情况下，路径选择仍会显著改变SGD收敛速度，并提出可数值求解的路径优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中路径 g_t 通常被视为设计选择，但既有工作多从生成质量或采样效率角度考虑，缺乏从优化角度分析路径如何影响训练收敛。作者希望回答：在FM目标完全相同时，路径的选择是否会影响SGD的收敛速度，以及如何选出最优路径。

Method: 作者考虑诱导相同边缘分布 p_t 与边缘速度场 v*_t（即相同FM目标）的路径类 G(p_t, v*_t)，分析随机梯度方差。(i) 对线性速度模型与一维高斯数据，推导SGD迭代复杂度的紧界（至对数因子），并给出线性路径中的解析最优路径；(ii) 将方差分析推广到一般FM问题，在固定 θ 下把路径选择形式化为带约束的方差最小化问题 PathOpt_θ；(iii) 由于约束 g_t∈G(p_t,v*_t) 难以直接验证，推导出可由样本估计的等价约束形式以便数值求解。

Result: 理论表明路径选择在FM目标相同时会从根本上改变SGD收敛率；在一维高斯线性情形下得到迭代复杂度的紧界及解析最优路径；PathOpt_θ 中保持路径类约束是必要的，忽略该约束单纯降方差反而会减慢收敛；等价样本可估计约束使路径可数值搜索。实验在高斯数据、高斯混合模型及真实数据集上验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 路径选择不仅是生成建模的设计细节，更是影响优化收敛的关键因素；在保持相同FM目标的前提下最小化随机梯度方差可加速训练，且所提等价约束形式使该方法具有实际可操作性。

Abstract: In flow matching (FM), a velocity model $v_θ$ is trained using a predefined path $g_t$ that connects data and noise samples (e.g., $g_t(x_0, x_1) = (1 - t) x_0 + t x_1$). In this work, we study the choice of this path from an optimization perspective by analyzing the variance of stochastic gradients. We consider the class $G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ of paths that induce the same marginal distributions $p_t$ and marginal velocity field $v^\star_t$, and therefore the same FM objective. Our main finding is that the choice of path $g_t$ can fundamentally change the convergence rate of SGD, even when the FM objective remains exactly the same. (i) For a linear velocity model and one-dimensional Gaussian data, we derive a tight bound on the SGD iteration complexity up to logarithmic factors and find an analytically optimal path that minimizes this bound among linear paths inducing the same FM problem. (ii) We then extend the variance analysis to general FM problems and formulate path selection at a fixed $θ$ as the variance-minimization problem PathOpt$_θ$, constrained to $g_t\in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$. We show that this constraint is essential: reducing variance without it can lead to slower convergence. (iii) Since the constraint $g_t \in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ cannot generally be verified directly, we derive an equivalent formulation with constraints that can be estimated from samples, allowing paths to be found numerically. Our theoretical results are supported by experiments with Gaussian data, Gaussian mixture models, and real datasets.

</details>


### [258] [Hybrid Variational Quantum Circuits for Multivariate Regression and High-Dimensional Data Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17358)
*Koffi Ognandon Ayena,Frédéric Holweck,Serge Iovleff,Amah S d'Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合变分量子电路（HVQC），在变分量子电路后加入经典仿射后处理层，实现向量值回归，并在多个数据集上取得优于XGBoost和随机森林、媲美高斯过程回归的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子电路（VQC）是经典优化的参数化量子电路，但进行向量值回归时通常需要多个独立标量电路，带来线性开销。作者希望扩展VQC结构，在避免这种开销的同时实现向量值回归。

Method: 提出混合变分量子电路（HVQC）：在VQC基础上增加一个经典仿射后测量层，实现向量值回归而无需多个独立标量电路。理论上证明基础的一量子比特和两量子比特电路可通过数据重上传和纠缠逼近二次函数及乘积，为整体架构提供基础。

Result: 在两个合成图像重建数据集和Friedman1基准（40,568个测试样本）上，HVQC与高斯过程回归相当，并优于XGBoost和随机森林。消融实验确认量子与经典组件均不可或缺，且特征映射在混合量子-经典模型中起核心作用。

Conclusion: HVQC通过结合量子电路与经典仿射层，有效实现向量值回归，性能可与高斯过程回归媲美，并强调了特征映射在混合模型中的关键地位。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are parameterized quantum circuits optimized classically. We propose a hybrid variational quantum circuit (HVQC) extending VQCs with a classical affine post-measurement layer, enabling vector-valued regression without the linear overhead of independent scalar circuits. Theoretically, we show that elementary one-and two-qubit circuits can approximate quadratic functions and products via data re-uploading and entanglement, providing the foundations of the full architecture. Experimentally, on two synthetic image reconstruction datasets and the Friedman1 benchmark (40,568 test samples), our HVQC matches Gaussian Process Regression and outperforms XGBoost and Random Forest. An ablation study confirms that both quantum and classical components are essential, and results highlight the central role of the feature map in hybrid quantum-classical models.

</details>


### [259] [Large Language Models Develop Belief State Geometry In-Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17376)
*Daniel Balcells,Andrew Jun Lee,Chirag Rastogi,Paul M. Riechers,Adam Shai,Xavier Poncini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究大模型上下文学习（ICL）所依赖的内部表征：在HMM生成数据的受控设定下，发现隐状态后验（信念状态）可从残差流激活中线性解码，且对相应子空间的干预具有功能相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通过下一词预测展现出强大的ICL能力，但支撑ICL的内部表征机制仍不清楚。作者希望以可控的实验设置，检验ICL是否近似于对上下文推断出的生成模型做最优贝叶斯预测。

Method: 用40个具有非平凡信念结构的隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）生成数据，提示6个开源LLM，并通过线性探针从残差流激活中解码对应信念状态（给定观察历史后隐状态的后验分布）；随后利用子空间修补与引导（patching/steering）干预探针识别的子空间，并设置对照实验。

Result: 信念状态可从残差流激活中线性解码，峰值探针R²达0.83–0.99（跨HMM与LLM组合），分布于从早到深的各层；干预探针子空间后下游预测质量与未干预模型相当，而对照干预则显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 在表征层面证明开源LLM的ICL近似于对上下文推断生成模型的最优贝叶斯预测，并将"输入分布结构—激活几何"的联系从显式训练于HMM的玩具网络扩展到生产级LLM。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on next-token prediction exhibit remarkable in-context learning (ICL) abilities, yet the representations that support ICL remain poorly understood. We consider such representations in a controlled setting: prompting LLMs with data emitted from hidden Markov models (HMMs) and probing for the corresponding belief state -- the posterior distribution over the HMM's hidden states given the observed token history. Across six open-source LLMs prompted with data from 40 HMMs selected for non-trivial belief structure, we find that belief states are linearly decodable from residual stream activations, with peak probe $R^2$-values from 0.83-0.99 across HMM and LLM combinations, ranging from early to late layers. To establish functional relevance, we intervene directly on the probe-identified subspace via patching and steering, resulting in downstream prediction quality on the order of the untampered model, while controls degrade performance substantially. Together, these results provide representation-level evidence that ICL in open-source LLMs approximates optimal Bayesian prediction over a context-inferred generative model. More broadly, our findings extend prior results linking input-distribution structure to activation geometry: from toy networks trained explicitly on HMM data to production-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [260] [OPEN-1B: A Fully Auditable Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17380)
*John Donaghy,Brian Wilcox,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Shikhar Rastogi,Adam St Arnaud,Alexey Titov,Jordan Greenberg,Ben Fielding,Harry Grieve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"完全可审计"的新透明度层级,通过消除训练中的非确定性来源,使大模型训练的每一步可在异构商用硬件上按位复现,并用集体验证方案实现整体训练过程的可核查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源语言模型虽公开权重、数据和配方,但因浮点运算的非结合性,无法证明其可复现。现有确定性模式仅限同一机器,跨硬件不可复现,导致存在未披露数据、注入偏见或后门无法被现有技术(如proof-of-learning、proof-of-training-data)排除。

Method: 对训练非确定性的三个来源施加确定顺序:GPU kernel归约、数据并行集群中的批次排序、节点间/节点内集合通信;从而可在单台商用硬件上重放任意单步并与公开轨迹比对。由于整轮训练无法在单机重放,采用集体验证方案,由多个独立审计者各自认证单步,合起来覆盖全程。

Result: 实现了每一步在所有数据样本上的操作可在异构商用硬件上以按位确定性独立复现;发布Open-1B模型及其完整预训练数据集、所有中间检查点、训练代码库和审计工具。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并实现了"完全可审计"的模型透明度层级,通过消除训练非确定性并结合集体验证,使大模型训练过程可被独立、按位地复现和核查。

Abstract: Open-source language models have a reproducibility problem. Despite releasing weights, training data, and recipes, none of them are provably reproducible due to the non-associativity of floating-point arithmetic. Deep learning frameworks often offer a deterministic execution mode, allowing reproducible operations on the same machines. Unfortunately, this determinism does not carry across hardware such that a user can verify that a released checkpoint was actually produced using the declared training recipe. This leaves room for undisclosed data, injected biases, or backdoors that existing techniques such as proof-of-learning or proof-of-training-data cannot rule out.
  We introduce a new tier of model transparency, fully auditable, in which every operation on every data sample during training is independently reproducible on heterogeneous commodity hardware with bitwise certainty. By imposing a definite order on the sources of training nondeterminism, GPU kernel reductions, data batch ordering across a data-parallel cluster, and inter/intra-node collective communication, we make it possible to replay any individual step of a large, distributed training run on a single piece of commodity hardware and check it against the published trajectory.
  Because replaying an entire run on one machine is infeasible, we support this with a collective verification scheme in which many independent auditors each certify individual steps, together covering the whole run. We release Open-1B, a model trained under this regime, together with its full pretraining dataset, every intermediate checkpoint, the training codebase, and the audit harness needed to reproduce and verify any step of its training.

</details>


### [261] [Bridging the Confidence Gap: Temperature Scaling for Calibrating Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17386)
*Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Yinuo Xu,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoTS和E-CoTS两种后处理校准方法，通过温度缩放缩小适应预测与零样本预测的置信度差距，在提升TPT校准性能的同时保持甚至提高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时提示调优（TPT）虽能提升单样本适应准确率，却损害了校准性能；现有校准方法通过正则化促使文本嵌入分散来降低校准误差，但往往导致准确率下降。

Method: 受零样本预测良好校准特性的启发，提出CoTS——对适应预测施加温度缩放，以最小化其与零样本预测之间的置信度差距；为充分利用多次增强，引入弱-强集成策略提升准确率，并将CoTS应用于该集成得到E-CoTS以维持良好校准。

Result: 在多种数据集和骨干网络上实验表明，E-CoTS将ImageNet变体上TPT的平均期望校准误差从11.90%降至5.38%，同时准确率从60.74%提升至62.95%；与现有校准方法结合时，通常能同时提升准确率与校准性能。

Conclusion: CoTS与E-CoTS能有效缓解误校准且不牺牲主干准确率，并可即插即用地增强现有校准方法。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) enables adaptation on a single test instance, achieving improved accuracy but often sacrificing calibration performance. Most existing calibration methods introduce additional regularization terms to promote dispersion across text embeddings and reduce calibration error, yet these methods often suffer from a drop in accuracy. Motivated by the well-calibrated nature of zero-shot predictions, we propose CoTS, a simple yet effective post-hoc calibration method that preserves accuracy. Specifically, CoTS applies temperature scaling to minimize the confidence gap between adapted and zero-shot predictions. To fully exploit the potential of multiple augmentations during adaptation, we introduce a weak-strong ensemble strategy that further boosts accuracy. We then apply CoTS to this ensemble, termed E-CoTS, to maintain its well-calibrated property. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets and backbones show that our approaches effectively mitigate miscalibration without compromising primary accuracy. For instance, E-CoTS reduces the average expected calibration error of TPT from 11.90% to 5.38% on ImageNet variants, while even increasing accuracy from 60.74% to 62.95%. Moreover, when integrated with existing calibration methods, E-CoTS usually enhances both accuracy and calibration simultaneously.

</details>


### [262] [Knowledge as Orbit: Finite Collections as Phases of an Exactly Periodic Latent Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17417)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用"单一闭合旋转动力学"内蕴存储有限知识：把 X 个项目编码为学得潜空间中固定旋转的第 i 个相位，用共享解码器解码所有相位，旋转取循环整数谐波（实离散傅里叶算子），保证 R^X = 恒等，精确闭合而非学出来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限知识通常以外延方式存储，每个条目一个编码或向量。本文追问：有限集合能否改为内蕴存储，即作为某个紧凑法则的解码轨道，且该法则能精确回到起点。其动机在于用代数结构替代独立实例的堆叠，实现精确周期性并降低潜存储开销。

Method: 在学得潜空间中用固定旋转编码第 i 个条目为第 i 个相位，共享网络解码所有相位；潜变量经整数谐波旋转库（实离散傅里叶算子）推进，使 R^X = 恒等。以图像为受控载体，循环视频作为相位顺序即内容时间结构的场景。控制实验：固定解码器只变算子，比较一般学习算子、保范但非周期算子、精确周期算子；并设码本对照、帧索引基线对照，以及把基线频率钉到循环谐波的验证。

Result: 一般学习算子发散，保范非周期算子在循环处退化，精确周期算子表现平坦；真实图像上差距更大。容量取决于解码器预算：稠密解码器每张清晰图有结构性开销，任何尺寸都无法与压缩调和；小型卷积解码器在共享流形的对象上可同时清晰与压缩。码本对照显示生成法则在重建意义上是免费的，却使潜存储成倍增加。七个基准片段上，与匹配的帧索引基线相比，循环在等参数下达到相当或更好的保真度，并以机器精度闭环，而基线留下可见接缝；把基线频率钉到循环谐波也能消除接缝，证实精确周期性是关键约束。

Conclusion: 有限循环知识可以作为动力学而非独立实例来存储：精确递推由代数保证，内容由共享解码器提供。精确周期性是使循环无缝、避免退化的有效约束。

Abstract: Finite knowledge is usually stored extensionally, one code or vector per item. We ask whether a finite collection can instead be stored intensionally, as the decoded orbit of one compact law that returns exactly to its start. For X objects, we encode item i as the i-th phase of a fixed rotation in a learned latent space and decode all phases with a shared network; the latent advances through a bank of rotations at integer harmonics of the cycle, a real discrete Fourier operator, so that R^X equals the identity and exact closure is guaranteed rather than learned. Images are a controlled carrier; looping video is the case where the phase order is the content's own temporal structure. Holding the decoder fixed and varying only the operator, a general learned operator diverges, a norm-preserving but non-periodic one degrades around the loop, and the exactly periodic operator is flat; on real images the gap widens. Capacity is then the decoder's budget: dense decoders carry a structural overhead per crisp image that no size reconciles with compression, while a small convolutional decoder on objects that share a manifold reaches crisp and compressed. A codebook control shows the generative law is free in reconstruction terms while multiplying the latent store many-fold. On seven benchmark clips, against a matched frame-index baseline, the cycle reaches equal or better fidelity at equal parameters while wrapping at machine precision, where the baseline leaves a visible seam; pinning the baseline's frequencies to loop harmonics closes its seam too, confirming that exact periodicity is the operative constraint. Finite cyclic knowledge can be stored as dynamics rather than independent instances, with exact recurrence supplied by algebra and content by a shared decoder.

</details>


### [263] [Learning-Guided Planning in Large Dynamic Action Spaces: Budgeted Tree Search for One-to-Many Mobile Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17429)
*Liang-Ching Tao,Pi-Chung Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LP-BTS是一种学习引导的规划架构，在动态、大规模候选动作空间（移动充电场景，动作数从736到2813不等）中，通过图提议策略、价值评论器和边预算PUCT进行决策，在密封确认集上取得最高存活率0.4545和alive-AUC 0.8031。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将当前状态映射到动作的学习型序列决策系统在候选动作众多、具有几何结构且随状态重建时变得脆弱。一对一多的移动充电场景即为此类典型：N=250传感器时初始状态约产生1,125个候选充电停靠动作，且动作空间随传感器死亡而变化。

Method: LP-BTS：图提议策略集中于小候选支撑集；学习型价值评论器评估叶节点；带边预算的PUCT在提交动作前比较短程模拟未来。由于策略对该集合打分而不依赖固定输出头，单一冻结检查点即可覆盖所有评估设置（动作规模736至2,813）。

Result: 匹配消融显示互补效应：均匀采样损失8.8个存活百分点；固定目标支撑后PUCT保留1.4个百分点（约250传感器中的3.5个），直接策略选择行程远23%。在预先指定、一次性评估的密封30场景确认库上，LP-BTS取得最高存活率0.4545与alive-AUC 0.8031。相对最强领域工程对比方法的存活优势估计为+0.0066（95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]），差异未达显著；但在每个配对场景上均超过截止时间启发式和两个源自直接策略的重建方法。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，结果为大规模动态动作空间中的学习引导规划提供了受控证据。两个学习行均为对Gong等人所报告变体的训练、源衍生重建。

Abstract: Many learned sequential decision systems map the current state directly to an action. That shortcut becomes brittle when candidate actions are numerous, geometrically structured, and rebuilt with the state. One-to-many mobile charging makes this setting concrete: with N=250 sensors, the initial state induces about 1,125 candidate charging-stop actions; each chosen stop simultaneously serves its in-range sensors, and the action universe changes as sensors die. LP-BTS is a learning-guided planning architecture: a graph proposal policy concentrates a small candidate support, a learned value critic evaluates leaves, and edge-budgeted PUCT compares short simulated futures before committing an action. Because the policy scores this set without a fixed output head, a single frozen checkpoint covers every evaluated setting, spanning action universes from 736 to 2,813 stops. Matched ablations reveal complementary effects: uniform sampling costs 8.8 survival percentage points, while, with targeted support fixed, PUCT jointly retains 1.4 points (about 3.5 of 250 sensors) and direct policy selection travels 23% farther. On a prospectively specified, sealed 30-scenario confirmatory bank evaluated once, LP-BTS attains the highest observed survival (0.4545) and alive-AUC (0.8031). Its estimated survival advantage over the strongest domain-engineered comparator is +0.0066 (95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]), an unresolved difference, while it exceeds a deadline heuristic and two source-derived direct-policy reconstructions on every paired scenario. Both learned rows are trained, source-derived reconstructions of variants reported by Gong et al. In this setting, the results provide controlled evidence about learning-guided planning in a large, dynamic action space.

</details>


### [264] [Reduced-Space Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization of Process Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17440)
*Niki Triantafyllou,Andrea Bernardi,Maria M. Papathanasiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种降维空间多保真贝叶斯优化（RS-MFBO）框架，用于高维昂贵黑箱函数优化，通过全局敏感性分析降维与保真度增强高斯过程，显著减少高保真仿真调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业过程流程优化常因严格仿真成本高昂和复杂设计空间维数灾难而计算代价过大，亟需降低计算成本的高效优化方法。

Method: 该框架整合全局敏感性分析（GSA）进行降维，构建保真度增强高斯过程以捕捉低保真近似与高保真评估间的相关性；并采用成本感知采集策略，配合冷却与提升机制，自适应分配不同保真度的样本。

Result: 在SuperPro Designer的质粒DNA生物过程和Aspen HYSYS的绿色燃料合成工厂两个工业仿真器上验证，多种经济与物理目标下均大幅减少高保真仿真评估次数，同时保持与单保真基线相当的优化性能。

Conclusion: RS-MFBO是一种可扩展、与仿真器无关的、面向成本受限黑箱优化的有效方法。

Abstract: Optimizing industrial process flowsheets is often computationally prohibitive due to the high cost of rigorous simulations and the curse of dimensionality inherent in complex design spaces. To address these challenges, we present a reduced-space multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (RS-MFBO) framework designed for high-dimensional, expensive black-box functions. The approach integrates Global Sensitivity Analysis (GSA) for dimensionality reduction with a fidelity-augmented Gaussian process that captures correlations between low-cost approximations and expensive high-fidelity evaluations. A cost-aware acquisition strategy, augmented with cooldown and promotion mechanisms, adaptively guides the allocation of samples across fidelities. The framework is validated on two distinct industrial process simulators: a plasmid DNA bioprocess in SuperPro Designer and a green fuel synthesis plant in Aspen HYSYS. Results across diverse economic and physical objectives demonstrate that the proposed method substantially reduces the number of high-fidelity simulator evaluations while maintaining competitive optimization performance compared to single-fidelity baselines. These results highlight RS-MFBO as a scalable, simulator-agnostic approach for cost-constrained black-box optimization.

</details>


### [265] [Coupled Calibration and Learning: Mitigating Teacher Bias in LLM Distillation without Target-Domain Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17474)
*Haichen Hu,Yuheng Zhang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CCL算法，通过耦合教师校准与学生更新，在无目标域奖励反馈下实现LLM蒸馏，理论上证明学生收敛到oracle学生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接模仿式LLM蒸馏会传递教师的系统性偏差和错误，在协变量偏移下尤为严重：此时教师对目标问题的可靠性不确定，且缺少目标域奖励反馈。

Method: CCL（Coupled Calibration and Learning）：通过token级分支将教师校准与学生更新耦合，仅使用源问题上的奖励反馈。每次迭代先用源反馈校准教师，再用校准后的教师训练学生在目标问题上，更新后的学生反过来指导后续校准。在自回归策略框架下进行理论分析。

Result: 证明输出学生到oracle学生的期望平均KL散度按迭代次数多项式速率收敛到零；量化了投影学生梯度更新的进展并控制了教师校准误差；进一步建立与正则化直接匹配的分离性：即使教师的正则化目标奖励高于所有学生策略，直接匹配相对oracle学生的误差仍可有界远离零。

Conclusion: LLM蒸馏可通过耦合校准与学习克服持续的教师偏差并恢复最优学生，且无需目标域奖励反馈。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) distillation aims to transfer the capabilities of a powerful teacher to a smaller student. Direct imitation, however, can also transfer the teacher's systematic bias and errors. This challenge is particularly pronounced under covariate shift, when the teacher's reliability on target questions is uncertain and target-domain reward feedback is unavailable. We propose Coupled Calibration and Learning (CCL), an LLM distillation algorithm that couples teacher calibration with student updates through token-level branching, using reward feedback only on source questions. Each iteration calibrates the teacher using source feedback and then uses the calibrated teacher to train the student on target questions. The updated student, in turn, informs subsequent calibration. In an autoregressive policy framework, we prove that the output student's expected average Kullback-Leibler divergence to the oracle student converges to zero at a polynomial rate in the number of iterations. The oracle maximizes the true reference-regularized target reward within the student class, which need not represent the unrestricted optimal policy. Our analysis quantifies the progress of projected student gradient updates while controlling the error in teacher calibration. We further establish a separation from regularized direct matching: its error relative to the oracle student can remain bounded away from zero even when the teacher achieves higher regularized target reward than every student policy. These results demonstrate that LLM distillation can overcome persistent teacher bias and recover the optimal student through coupled calibration and learning, without target-domain reward feedback.

</details>


### [266] [FreqSpaNet: Frequency and Spatial Learning of SFPF for Physical Layer Hardware Integrity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17491)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,YiZhe Wang,Meng Zhang,Xian Li,Yuying Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FreqSpaNet，一种针对空频极化指纹（SFPF）的表示学习网络，用于开放集硬件异常检测，以识别未授权硬件替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的硬件替换可在保持设备逻辑身份不变的同时改变其物理实现，威胁硬件完整性验证。SFPF 虽能捕获设备相关的多频多方向响应，但其频率维度与空间维度具有不同的结构依赖性，需要分别建模。

Method: FreqSpaNet 采用双分支结构：频率分支捕获相邻频率间的局部变化；几何感知空间分支利用角度信息建模方向关系。两路表示通过自适应融合结合，并辅以互补预训练，在捕获共享信息的同时保留频率与空间表示的各自特性。

Result: FreqSpaNet 取得 96.31% 的平均 AUROC，较基线提升 9.05 个百分点；在七种硬件替换场景下的结果进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: FreqSpaNet 通过区分建模频率与空间结构并自适应融合，有效提升了开放集硬件异常检测性能，适用于多种硬件替换场景。

Abstract: Unauthorized hardware replacement can preserve a wireless device's logical identity while altering its physical implementation, posing a challenge to hardware integrity verification. Spatio-frequency polarization fingerprints (SFPFs) capture device-dependent responses across multiple frequencies and directions, but their frequency and spatial dimensions exhibit different structural dependencies. We propose FreqSpaNet, an SFPF representation learning network for open set hardware anomaly detection. A frequency branch captures local variations among neighboring frequencies, while a geometry-aware spatial branch models directional relationships using angular information. The two representations are combined through adaptive fusion, and complementary pretraining further captures shared information while preserving the distinct characteristics of the frequency and spatial representations. Experiments show that FreqSpaNet achieves a mean AUROC of 96.31\%, 9.05 points above the baseline. Results under seven hardware replacement scenarios further verify the effectiveness of FreqSpaNet.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Bi-MoDe: Bilateral Control-based Imitation Learning via Modifier-Conditioned Decoding for Modulation of Execution Speed and Contact Intensity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16040)
*Takumi Kobayashi,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Bi-MoDe，一种基于修饰符条件解码的模仿学习框架，可将行为指令注入 Transformer 动作解码器，实现接触丰富任务中时间与物理风格的可控执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边控制模仿学习能同时捕捉位置与力信息，适合接触丰富操作，但现有方法难以让操作者在推理时指定任务执行方式（如快慢、轻重），缺乏行为层面的可控性。

Method: 提出 Bi-MoDe，通过 adaLN-Zero 将受约束的潜在变量注入 Transformer 动作解码器的每一层，使行为指令直接影响动作块生成；在真实擦白板任务上，结合时间与物理修饰符进行评估，并进行了解码器条件与潜在空间组合的消融实验。

Result: Bi-MoDe 在物理指令跟随方面优于动作分块基线，同时保持相当的时间控制能力；消融显示解码器条件与潜在空间组合存在交互作用，两者结合对准确跟随物理指令至关重要。

Conclusion: 将修饰符条件注入解码器各层并结合潜在空间组合，可有效提升模仿学习的行为可控性，尤其在物理风格指令跟随方面，为接触丰富操作提供更灵活的人机交互方式。

Abstract: Bilateral control-based imitation learning captures both position and force information, making it well suited to contact-rich manipulation. However, existing approaches provide limited means for an operator to specify how a learned task should be executed at inference time, such as slowly or quickly, gently or firmly. We propose Bi-MoDe, a modifier-conditioned decoding framework that injects a constrained latent into every layer of the Transformer action decoder via adaLN-Zero, allowing behavioral directives to directly influence action-chunk generation. We evaluate the method on a real-world whiteboard wiping task with combinations of temporal and physical modifiers. Bi-MoDe improves physical directive following over the action-chunking baseline while maintaining comparable temporal control. An ablation further shows that decoder conditioning and latent-space composition interact, and that their combination is important for accurate physical directive following. Additional material is available at the https://mertcookimg.github.io/bi-mode/

</details>


### [268] [MR-GLi: Mixed Reality-Based Gripper-Linked Overlays for Underwater Robot Arm Teleoperation via Bilateral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16041)
*Masashi Sasago,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出MR-GLi混合现实界面，将反作用力矩指示器与腕部相机图像空间配准到机器人夹爪上，用于水下双边遥操作。20名受试者与2D显示器对比，结果表明MR-GLi在力矩调节性能上与2D显示器相当，但主观负担更低，说明夹爪关联式MR叠加具有可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉力矩反馈可支持水下双边遥操作，但混合现实（MR）相比传统显示器呈现方式是否更具优势尚不明确。研究旨在探究两者在信息获取与任务表现上的差异。

Method: 设计MR-GLi界面，将反作用力矩指示器和腕部相机图像空间注册到机器人夹爪。20名受试者在被试内平衡设计下，分别使用MR和2D显示器完成刚性与柔性物体的举升与拾放任务，视觉反馈内容与四通道双边控制保持一致。

Result: MR-GLi提供了与夹爪关联的视觉反馈访问方式，力矩调节性能与2D显示器相当；主观评价显示，在工作区与视觉反馈之间转移注意力所带来的感知负担降低。

Conclusion: 夹爪关联式MR叠加用于水下双边遥操作具有可行性，并强调除任务表现外还需考虑信息获取方式的重要性。

Abstract: Visual torque feedback supports underwater bilateral teleoperation, but the benefit of mixed reality (MR) over conventional monitor presentation remains unclear. We present MR-GLi, an MR interface that spatially registers a reaction torque indicator and wrist-camera image to the robot gripper. Twenty participants performed lift and pick-and-place tasks with rigid and compliant objects in a counterbalanced within-subject comparison with a 2D monitor, using identical visual-feedback content and four-channel bilateral control. MR-GLi provided gripper-linked access to visual feedback while maintaining a similar level of torque-regulation performance to the 2D monitor. Subjective evaluation further indicated reduced perceived burden associated with shifting attention between the workspace and visual feedback. These results demonstrate the feasibility of gripper-linked MR overlays for underwater bilateral teleoperation and highlight the importance of considering information access in addition to task performance. Additional material: https://mertcookimg.github.io/mr-gli/

</details>


### [269] [World-Action Models for Robot Learning and Control: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16074)
*Zuxing Lu,Hongjia Zhai,Guanzhi Wang,Huajian Zeng,Jiaqi Yang,Jingyu Liu,Lei Cheng,Yuantai Zhang,Yuheng Qiu,Zezhou Cheng,Ivan Laptev,Danfei Xu,Benjamin Riviere,Giuseppe Loianno,Eric Xing,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇面向机器人领域的综述，系统回顾了"世界-动作模型"（WAMs），它将对未来世界的预测与可执行动作生成相结合，并覆盖了定义、分类、应用、评测与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在开放环境中面临部分可观测、物理约束和动态任务背景，仅将观测与语言指令映射到动作是不够的，还需预判候选动作如何影响未来状态及任务结果。世界模型、视频生成与VLA策略的进展催生了世界-动作模型，但领域亟需系统性梳理。

Method: 本综述面向机器人领域，首先厘清了WAM与传统世界模型、基于模型的强化学习、动作条件视频生成、反应式VLA策略之间的关系；随后按统一分类法组织现有方法，维度包括表征、转移建模、动作接口、架构、训练流程、数据模态和扩展策略。

Result: 综述回顾了WAM在操作、导航和自动驾驶中的应用，并汇总了用于评测WAM系统的数据集、基准、指标与协议。

Conclusion: 讨论的关键挑战包括动作对齐、世界-动作解耦、空间与多视角一致性、长时序记忆、面向闭环策略学习的神经仿真以及高效推理。整体目标是为将预测性世界建模与动作生成相融合提供技术基础，推动更可靠的具身机器人智能。

Abstract: Robots operating in open environments act under partial observability, physical constraints, and dynamic task contexts. Beyond mapping observations and language instructions to actions, they must anticipate how candidate actions may affect future states and task-relevant outcomes. Recent advances in world models, video generation, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have motivated the development of World-Action Models (WAMs), which couple future world prediction with executable action generation. This survey provides a robotics-oriented review of WAMs. We clarify their scope relative to conventional world models, model-based reinforcement learning, action-conditioned video generation, and reactive VLA policies, and organize existing methods through a unified taxonomy covering representations, transition modeling, action interfaces, architectures, training pipelines, data modalities, and scaling strategies. We further review applications of WAMs in manipulation, navigation, and autonomous driving, and we summarize the datasets, benchmarks, metrics, and protocols used to evaluate WAM systems. Finally, we discuss key challenges in action alignment, world-action factorization, spatial and multi-view consistency, long-horizon memory, neural simulation for closed-loop policy learning, and efficient inference. Taken together, this survey aims to provide a concise technical foundation for integrating predictive world modeling with action generation, toward more reliable embodied robot intelligence. Project page: https://rcl-robotics.github.io/Awesome-World-Action-Models.

</details>


### [270] [AssemblyGrid v1: A Benchmark for Multi-Robot Production with Temporary Coalitions, Local Information, and Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16075)
*Fouad Bahrpeyma,David Heik,Dirk Reichelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AssemblyGrid v1，一个可复现的多机器人柔性生产基准，集成了流程推进、物料流转、临时协作与并发执行，并支持集中式与去中心化方法的统一评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性机器人生产需要同时决策流程推进、物料路由、资源分配、临时协作与并发执行，各决策相互影响可行性。去中心化控制下机器人仅有局部信息，系统进展依赖集体决策、共享资源与工位兼容性，与部分可观测和资源竞争下的协同多智能体决策高度相似，但缺少统一、可复现的基准。

Method: 构建 AssemblyGrid v1 任务级基准，整合显式流程推进、去中心化观测、物料转移、临时多机器人联盟、生产并发与依赖几何的可行性；设计 Flow、Coalition、Concurrency 三类工作负载，每类含三个场景等级；任务成功与评价指标独立于学习奖励与求解方法，使学习型与非学习型方法可在同一问题下比较；通过可执行一致性检查、机制研究与算法实验进行评估，采用特权集中式参考、结构化去中心化控制器及 IPPO、MAPPO、QMIX 等 MARL 方法。

Result: 一致性检查与机制研究验证了基准的可执行性；集中式与去中心化控制下均实现有效生产执行；MARL 实验表明去中心化策略能够仅凭局部观测与动作学到有效的生产行为。

Conclusion: AssemblyGrid v1 可作为受控基准，用于研究柔性机器人生产中的协同决策，并支持学习型与非学习型方法在同一任务上公平比较。

Abstract: Flexible robotic production requires joint decisions on process progression, material routing, resource assignment, temporary cooperation, and simultaneous execution, since each decision can affect the feasibility of the others. The challenge is greater under decentralized control, where each robot acts from bounded local information while system progress depends on collective decisions, shared resources, material state, and workspace compatibility. These properties closely match cooperative multi-agent decision making under partial observability and resource contention. This paper introduces AssemblyGrid v1, a reproducible benchmark for repeated multi-robot production that combines explicit process progression, decentralized observations, material transfer, temporary multi-robot coalitions, productive concurrency, and geometry-dependent feasibility within one task-level formulation. The benchmark includes Flow, Coalition, and Concurrency workload families, each with three scenario levels. Task success and evaluation measures are defined independently of learning reward and solution method, allowing learning-based and non-learning methods to address the same production problem. AssemblyGrid v1 is evaluated through executable conformance checks, mechanism studies, and algorithmic experiments using a privileged centralized reference, structured decentralized controllers, and MARL methods including IPPO, MAPPO, and QMIX. Results demonstrate productive execution under centralized and decentralized control. The MARL experiments further show that decentralized policies can learn effective production behavior from local observations and actions, supporting AssemblyGrid as a controlled benchmark for studying cooperative decision making in flexible robotic production.

</details>


### [271] [Structure-Preserving Quantum Circuit Architectures for Robot Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16089)
*Andrea Morghen,Pierluigi Arpenti,Roberto Schiattarella,Giovanni Acampora,Bruno Siciliano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种用于刚体变换的量子表示与电路架构，并专门应用于串联开链机械臂的Denavit–Hartenberg运动学：以单量子比特Bloch矢量编码带符号单位方向、经典度量幅值承载尺度，通过选择寄存器与读出比特复现末端位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化空间数据需要量子编码既能保持几何关系、暴露可测可观测量，又能在有限深度硬件上实现；本文旨在为刚体变换（特别是串联开链机械臂DH运动学）建立这样一种几何可解释、可实现的量子表示。

Method: 将每个平移贡献分解为经典度量幅值与由单量子比特Bloch矢量编码的带符号单位方向；参数化旋转复现坐标系的顺序传播。用选择寄存器按贡献幅值准备概率，指定读出量子比特的约化态编码归一化加权和，再由保留的经典尺度重建度量的末端位置；另两个读出比特编码末端坐标系轴。给出具有不同量子比特数、电路深度、受控操作与测量需求权衡的多种电路架构。

Result: 在理想期望值层面，测量的Pauli可观测量可复现相应经典运动学量；在串联机械臂上验证时，理想仿真下的位置与姿态重建误差数值上可忽略。有限次采样仿真、含噪执行、转译分析与硬件演示进一步刻画了统计误差、噪声敏感性与实现开销。

Conclusion: 该表示提供了紧凑且几何可解释的位姿接口，能复现经典运动学量，并在真实硬件实现中具备可执行的实现路径；作者明确不主张计算优势，仅强调表示、可测量性与实现可行性的刻画。

Abstract: Structured spatial data require quantum encodings that preserve geometric relations, expose measurable observables, and remain implementable on finite-depth hardware. This work introduces a quantum representation and circuit architecture for rigid-body transformations and specializes it to Denavit--Hartenberg kinematics of serial open-chain manipulators. Each translational contribution is factorized into a classical metric magnitude and a signed unit direction encoded by a single-qubit Bloch vector, while parameterized rotations reproduce the ordered propagation of frame directions. A selector register prepares probabilities proportional to the contribution magnitudes, and the reduced state of a designated readout qubit encodes their normalized weighted sum. The retained classical scale then reconstructs the metric end-effector position. Two additional readout qubits encode terminal-frame axes, providing a compact and geometrically interpretable pose interface. At the ideal expectation-value level, measured Pauli observables reproduce the corresponding classical kinematic quantities. Alternative circuit architectures realize the same representation with different tradeoffs in qubit count, circuit depth, controlled operations, and measurement requirements. Validation on a serial manipulator yields numerically negligible position and orientation reconstruction errors under ideal simulation. Finite-shot simulations, noisy executions, transpilation analysis, and a hardware demonstration further characterize statistical error, noise sensitivity, and implementation overhead without asserting computational advantage.

</details>


### [272] [Occupancy Network-Guided Autonomous Robotic Partial Nephrectomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16186)
*Ethan Kilmer,Pit Henrich,Jiawei Ge,Paul M. Scheikl,Laura Connolly,Soum D. Lokeshwar,Joseph Chen,Justin D. Opfermann,Kaitlyn Kumar,Lauren Shepard,Ahmed Ghazi,Nirmish Singla,Richard J. Cha,Kevin Cleary,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Axel Krieger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个视觉引导的自主软组织肿瘤切除系统，可在离体水凝胶模型中完成完整肾部分切除并实现阴性切缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主软组织癌症手术仅能作用于器官表面，因为系统无法在组织变形或被切割后感知并适应解剖结构变化。

Method: 集成条件占用网络（完全在基于物理的仿真中训练），从单视角部分点云推断完整三维解剖（肿瘤、边缘组织和肾脏）；结合深度相机采集表面点云、双臂机器人（电外科切割与真空组织操控）以及自主切除控制策略，实现自适应规划与执行，并在组织被切割变形时保持术中跟踪。

Result: 在患者来源的水凝胶模型、开放肾部分切除条件下，机器人完成8次连续自主肿瘤切除、共77次电外科切割，全部切缘阴性，平均绝对切缘误差为1.61±0.48 mm。

Conclusion: 首次展示了监督式自主闭环、影像驱动、切缘阴性肿瘤切除的基础框架（模型验证阶段）。

Abstract: Autonomous soft-tissue cancer surgery has been limited to interventions on organ surfaces, because current systems cannot perceive and adapt to anatomy once it deforms or is cut. We introduce the first vision-guided autonomous system capable of performing complete tumor resections for partial nephrectomy. Our system integrates conditional occupancy networks, trained entirely in a physics-based simulation, that infer full 3-D anatomy (tumor, margin tissue, and kidney) from single-view partial point clouds. These occupancy networks maintain intraoperative tracking even as tissue is cut and deformed, enabling adaptive planning and execution. The surgical platform combines a depth camera for capturing surface point clouds, dual robotic arms for electrosurgical cutting and vacuum-based tissue manipulation, and an autonomous control strategy for tumor resection. In patient-derived hydrogel phantoms under an open partial nephrectomy setting, the robot performed eight consecutive autonomous tumor resections comprising 77 electrosurgical cuts, with all cuts achieving negative surgical margins and 1.61 $\pm$ 0.48 mm mean absolute margin error. This work demonstrates, for the first time, a foundation for supervised autonomous closed-loop, imaging-driven, margin-negative tumor removal in phantoms.

</details>


### [273] [Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot with Modular Stiffness and In-Situ Self Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16256)
*Guo Ning,Sue,Zheng Cao,Junzhe Hu,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Tiancheng Wu,Zackory Erickson,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自包含模块化连续体机器人平台，通过可互换关节的机械重构与基于磁性传感器的本体感知学习框架，实现无需外部追踪的自感知与构型自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续体机器人大多针对特定任务设计，且依赖外部传感基础设施进行形状与位姿感知，导致适应性差、难以在实际场景中部署。因此需要一种自包含、可重构且具备本体感知能力的连续体机器人平台。

Method: 机器人由可互换的连续体关节构成，关节刚度经解析预计算，支持快速组装并可直接编程机器人形状；采用磁性传感器实现本体感知，并构建模块化学习框架，每个关节仅训练单一模型并可跨不同构型复用。

Result: 在真实环境中完成实验验证，系统展示出本体自感知能力，并能在无外部追踪的条件下实现形状与构型的自适应。

Conclusion: 该模块化连续体机器人平台结合机械可重构性与模块化学习式本体感知，摆脱对外部传感设施的依赖，提升了连续体机器人的适应性与实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Continuum robots enable smooth shape morphing and safe interaction in confined environments. However, most existing systems are task-specific and depend on external sensing infrastructure, limiting their adaptability and real-world deployment. This paper presents a self-contained modular continuum robotic platform that combines mechanical reconfigurability with onboard pose estimation. The robot is constructed from interchangeable continuum joints with analytically precomputed stiffness, allowing rapid assembly and direct programming of the robot shape. Proprioceptive sensing is achieved using magnetic sensors and a modular learning-based framework, where a single model is trained per joint and reused across configurations. The system is experimentally validated in real world, demonstrating self-sensing capabilities and adaptation without external tracking.

</details>


### [274] [ConGraspXL: Controllable Constraint-Conditioned Dexterous Grasping Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16319)
*Hui Zhang,Mirko Meboldt,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ConGraspXL，在GraspXL基础上扩展出可控的、以约束条件驱动的抓取动作生成方法，支持多种任务约束及其组合，并提供即插即用的底层抓取控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取通常面向特定任务，涉及逼近方向、期望接触区域、腕部轨迹、功能性手部姿态等异构约束。作者此前的GraspXL虽能对多样物体和手部形态实现可扩展的抓取动作合成，但缺乏在这些任务驱动约束下进行合成的可控性。

Method: ConGraspXL在GraspXL基础上扩展，实现受约束条件控制的可控抓取动作合成：引入分层约束表示，借助掩码残差接口实现灵活的约束组合，并通过动态手部中心与前馈腕部引导提升控制精度。

Result: 在保持GraspXL强泛化能力的同时，ConGraspXL对各类单项约束及其组合均能实现精确、灵活的可控性，可作为即插即用的底层抓取控制器，服务于全身抓取补全、功能性抓取和人体动作模仿等下游应用。

Conclusion: ConGraspXL通过约束条件驱动的可控合成，弥补了原有方法在任务约束下可控性不足的问题，为下游任务提供灵活通用的底层抓取控制方案。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping is usually conducted for specific tasks, leading to heterogeneous constraints such as specific approach directions, desired contact regions, specified wrist trajectories, and functional hand poses. Our previous work, GraspXL, achieves scalable grasping motion synthesis for diverse objects and hand morphologies, while lacking controllability for synthesis under such various task-driven constraints. In this paper, we propose ConGraspXL, which extends GraspXL with controllable constraint-conditioned grasp motion synthesis that accommodates diverse task-driven constraints and their combinations. We introduce a hierarchical constraint formulation, enable flexible constraint composition with a masked residual interface, and improve control precision with dynamic hand centers and feed-forward wrist guidance. Without losing the strong generalization capabilities of GraspXL, ConGraspXL enables precise and flexible controllability for various individual constraints and their combinations, providing a plug-and-play low-level grasp controller for downstream applications such as whole-body grasp completion, functional grasping, and human-motion imitation.

</details>


### [275] [ManiSkillFormer: Demonstration-Free Compositional Manipulation via Task-Conditioned Geometric Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16331)
*Peiqi Yu,Mosam Dabhi,Shangtao Li,Bowei Li,Laszlo Jeni,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiSkillFormer是一个无需演示、可组合的神经符号机器人操作框架，通过任务条件几何契约显式结构化感知-动作接口，在双臂机器人上取得优于基线的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端视觉运动策略学习需要大量演示且难以复用与组合，作者希望实现无需演示、可跨物体与任务组合复用的操作能力。

Method: 提出任务条件几何契约，每个操作技能声明所需的语义几何基元（如关键点、表面法线）；LLM智能体基于人类定义的技能结构生成契约与运动模板；契约引导感知模块从观测中定位任务相关的3D基元，用于实例化存储在技能库中的可复用运动模板。

Result: 在Galaxea R1-Lite双臂机器人上评测：8类30个实例的零样本抓放达88.24%成功率；功能操作（旋拧、倾倒、按压、折叠）平均75.00%；3个长时程任务完成率50–80%，均高于基线与两个消融流程。

Conclusion: 该设计实现了跨物体与任务的可组合、可复用操作，无需针对单个物体微调策略或额外机器人演示。

Abstract: We present ManiSkillFormer, a neuro-symbolic framework for demonstration-free and compositional robotic manipulation. Instead of learning end-to-end visuomotor policies, ManiSkillFormer introduces task-conditioned geometric contracts that explicitly structure the interface between perception and action. Each manipulation skill declares the semantic geometric primitives required for execution, such as object keypoints and surface normals. Building on human-defined skill structures, LLM agents generate these contracts and corresponding motion templates for different objects and task contexts. These contracts guide the perception module to ground task-relevant 3D primitives from observations, which are then used to instantiate reusable motion templates stored in a skill library. We evaluate ManiSkillFormer on Galaxea R1-Lite dual-arm robot across three settings: zero-shot pick-and-place over 8 object categories with 30 different instances, functional manipulation tasks including unscrewing, pouring, pressing, and folding, and 3 long-horizon tasks. ManiSkillFormer achieves higher average success rates than the evaluated baselines and two ablated pipelines: 88.24% for demonstration-free pick-and-place, 75.00% average success on functional manipulation and 50--80% completion rates across the long-horizon tasks. These results show that our design enables composable and reusable manipulation across objects and tasks without per-object policy fine-tuning or additional robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [276] [Auto-HSI: Personalized human control of a robot swarm on demand by using LLMs for online automatic code generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16346)
*Alessandro Nazzari,Nathan Cerisara,Dorian Tonnis,Raina Zakir,Lorenzo Labarile,Weixu Zhu,Marco Dorigo,Mary Katherine Heinrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Auto-HSI方法，能根据自然语言描述与手势演示自动生成个性化的人-集群交互界面，无需编程即可控制机器人群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让未经训练的操作者也能通过自然语言描述和手势演示，定义机器人对其手势的集体响应行为，从而降低人-集群交互界面的开发门槛。

Method: 基于用户输入自动生成个性化状态机代码，实现集中式控制界面，支持单手与双手手势，用于遥操作机器人运动、队形与形变；并对跟踪与代码生成模块进行基准测试及全系统实验。

Result: 在物理模拟器中，真实操作者集中控制50个模拟机器人，在正常与噪声条件下完成进球、需形变的迷宫穿行、分队同时进两球等任务；操作中可实时更新个性化界面，并演示了真实机器人的实机运行。

Conclusion: Auto-HSI证明可按需自动生成可用的个性化人-集群交互界面，支持实时修改并适用于仿真与真实机器人场景。

Abstract: This paper presents Auto-HSI, a method for generating personalized human-swarm interaction (HSI) interfaces on demand. The objective is to enable untrained operators to use natural language descriptions and gesture demonstrations to explain how they want the robots to collectively behave in response to their gestures. Based on these inputs, the code should automatically be generated for personalized state machines that will control the robots as desired, in response to the desired gesture inputs. In the developed Auto-HSI prototype, the generated code produces a personalized interface for centralized control using one- and two-handed gestures, enabling a user to teleoperate the robots' motion, formation shape, and shape deformation. We test the gesture tracking and code generation components of Auto-HSI against performance benchmarks. We then test the full Auto-HSI prototype in ``live'' operation experiments, in which real human operators centrally control 50 simulated robots in a physics-based simulator, under nominal and noisy conditions. In these experiments, robots are teleoperated to: score a goal, traverse a maze that requires shape deformation, and score two simultaneous goals by splitting into two groups. We also demonstrate a real human operator making live updates to their personalized Auto-HSI interface during operation (in simulation). Finally, we demonstrate live operation of real robots.

</details>


### [277] [UDAV: Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16368)
*Ghazal Farhani,Shabnam Shabani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UDAV利用随机轨迹预测的离散度估计不确定性，在不确定性过高时触发重新考虑，从而提升无人机引导无人地面车辆越野导航的路径规划精度与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）可直接从航拍图像生成越野导航路径，但其预测缺乏可靠性指示。本文旨在为VLM路径规划提供可操作的置信度信号，以便选择性缓解大幅规划误差。

Method: UDAV（不确定性驱动的自适应VLM航点规划器）：对同一查询进行多次随机轨迹预测，选择其中心点（medoid）作为自洽的名义路径，并以预测间的空间离散度估计预测不确定性；当内部航点最大不确定性超过阈值时，触发“重新考虑”阶段，否则直接返回中心点结果。

Result: 在两次无人机飞行采集的400条留出轨迹查询上评估：随机中心点选择将平均位移误差（ADE）从确定性预测的147.4像素降至115.9像素；完整规划器平均ADE为110.4像素，相对确定性规划降低25.1%，且对所有查询均生成有效轨迹。UDAV在包括更高预算K=10共识基线在内的所有配置中取得最低的90分位与95分位误差；相对K=5中心点，这些误差从225.3和326.0像素降至199.0和290.8像素。

Conclusion: 随机VLM预测既能提供更强的名义路径，也能提供可操作的不确定性信号，用于选择性缓解大幅规划误差，从而提升越野导航路径规划的精度与可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate routes directly from aerial imagery for off-road navigation, but their predictions provide no indication of reliability. We present UDAV, an Uncertainty-Driven Adaptive VLM Waypoint Planner for UAV-guided UGV navigation. UDAV draws multiple stochastic trajectory predictions, selects their medoid as a self-consistent nominal route, and estimates predictive uncertainty from their spatial dispersion. When the maximum uncertainty across interior waypoints exceeds a threshold, UDAV invokes a reconsideration stage; otherwise, it returns the medoid directly. We evaluate UDAV on 400 held-out trajectory queries from two UAV flights. Stochastic medoid selection reduces the mean average displacement error (ADE) from 147.4 pixels for a deterministic prediction to 115.9 pixels. The complete planner achieves a mean ADE of 110.4 pixels, a 25.1% reduction relative to deterministic planning, while producing valid trajectories for all queries. UDAV also yields the lowest 90th- and 95th-percentile errors among all evaluated configurations, including a higher-budget K=10 consensus baseline. Relative to the K=5 medoid, UDAV reduces these errors from 225.3 and 326.0 pixels to 199.0 and 290.8 pixels, respectively. These results demonstrate that stochastic VLM predictions provide both a stronger nominal route and an actionable uncertainty signal for selectively mitigating large planning errors.

</details>


### [278] [Geometry vs Structure: Graph-Based Diagnostics for LiDAR Point-Cloud Simulation Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16378)
*Ghazal Farhani,Taufiq Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的框架，通过Louvain社区检测与图谱度量 $r_\lambda$ 评估仿真LiDAR点云的结构保真度，并在真实(Velodyne VLP-32C)与仿真(CARLA)数据上验证其与几何度量的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生是验证自动驾驶与ADAS传感器流程的可扩展低成本手段，但其保真度量化困难。尤其对3D LiDAR点云，传统几何度量（如Chamfer距离）仅捕捉逐点几何相似性，忽略了连通性、拓扑结构与物体级组织等关键结构差异。

Method: 构建基于图的评估框架：从真实与仿真点云构建图，采用Louvain社区检测识别空间连贯子图，并通过质心邻近度匹配对应社区；对每个匹配对计算有界图谱度量 $r_\lambda$（受Weyl不等式启发），并与密度感知Chamfer距离(CDC)作为几何基线对比。通过受控扰动实验验证度量性质。

Result: 扰动实验表明 $r_\lambda$ 对刚性变换不变、对传感器噪声鲁棒，同时保持对结构形变的敏感性。在50对真实(Velodyne VLP-32C)与仿真(CARLA)LiDAR扫描上评估，数据集涵盖车辆、植被、树木、建筑墙面四类，包含超过1,000个匹配社区，结果显示几何度量与结构度量捕捉了仿真保真度的互补方面。

Conclusion: 图谱分析可作为验证ADAS与自动驾驶数字孪生的额外诊断层，与几何度量共同构成更全面的仿真保真度评估体系。

Abstract: Digital twins provide a scalable and cost-effective complement to real-world testing for validating autonomous-driving and advanced driver-assistance system (ADAS) sensor pipelines. However, quantifying their fidelity remains challenging, particularly for 3D LiDAR point clouds, where conventional geometric metrics may overlook important structural discrepancies. We present a graph-based framework for evaluating the structural fidelity of simulated LiDAR point clouds against real-world scans. While scan-level metrics such as Chamfer distance capture point-wise geometric similarity, they do not explicitly represent connectivity, topology, or object-level organization. Our framework constructs graphs from real and simulated point clouds, applies Louvain community detection to identify spatially coherent subgraphs, and matches corresponding communities using centroid proximity. For each matched pair, we compute $r_λ$, a bounded graph-spectral metric motivated by Weyl's inequality, and compare it with density-aware Chamfer distance (CDC) as a geometric baseline. Controlled perturbation experiments demonstrate that $r_λ$ is invariant to rigid transformations and robust to sensor noise while remaining sensitive to structural deformation. We evaluate the framework on 50 paired real and simulated LiDAR scans acquired using a Velodyne VLP-32C sensor and CARLA, respectively. The dataset contains more than 1,000 matched communities across four representative classes: vehicles, vegetation, trees, and building walls. The results show that geometric and structural measures capture complementary aspects of simulation fidelity, supporting graph-spectral analysis as an additional diagnostic layer for validating digital twins in ADAS and autonomous-driving applications.

</details>


### [279] [Collision-Aware Humanoid Whole-Body Control under Imperfect Tracking Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16405)
*Mohitvishnu S. Gadde,Ashish Malik,Pranay Dugar,Aayam Kumar Shrestha,Alan Fern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RECAL——一种机器人-环境交叉注意力层，包裹在盲式全身控制器外，利用外部场景几何在目标跟踪与碰撞避免间权衡，支持浮基、末端执行器及持物命令，并在仿真和Digit V3真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人全身控制器（WBC）能跟踪目标并保持平衡，但大多"看不见"场景几何。当感知、规划或遥操作导致目标运动在几何上不安全时，容易发生碰撞。因此需要让盲式WBC具备几何感知的碰撞规避能力。

Method: RECAL将机器人、持物和环境表示为点云，通过机器人/物体点与环境之间的交叉注意力，生成几何感知的控制特征，以包裹（wrap）盲式WBC的方式实现碰撞感知跟踪，覆盖浮基与末端执行器命令及持物避碰。

Result: 在仿真中，相比其他几何感知WBC架构，RECAL在冻结臂/自适应臂运动、持物行走、站立操作等场景下提升了碰撞避免效果，同时保持目标跟踪性能。并在真实Digit V3人形机器人上进行了演示。

Conclusion: RECAL以模块化方式为盲式全身控制器赋予场景几何感知能力，在不牺牲跟踪性能的前提下提升碰撞安全性，并具备真机部署可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots often execute motion commands through whole-body controllers (WBCs) that track targets while maintaining balance and stability. However, most WBCs are blind to scene geometry, which can lead to collisions from imperfect target motions that are geometrically unsafe due to perception, planning, or teleoperation errors. We propose RECAL, a Robot--Environment Cross-Attention Layer that wraps a blind WBC to trade off target tracking against collision avoidance using external scene geometry. RECAL supports collision-aware tracking of floating-base and end-effector commands, including collision avoidance for held objects. It represents the robot, held objects, and environment as point clouds, using cross-attention between robot/object points and the environment to produce geometry-aware control features. In simulation, RECAL improves collision avoidance while preserving target-tracking performance across frozen-arm and adaptive-arm locomotion, object-carrying, and standing-manipulation scenarios relative to alternative geometry-aware WBC architectures. We further demonstrate the controller on a real Digit V3 humanoid robot.

</details>


### [280] [XRoboToolKit-T: Teleoperation with High Stability and Precision with Tactile Sensing for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16437)
*Xiwen Dengxiong,Xueting Wang,Ke Jing,Rui Li,Yunbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出带触觉驱动的遥操作系统XRoboToolKit-T，通过触觉力控架构实现高频稳定的富接触操作，提升数据采集效率与操作稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富接触操作任务需要高质量机器人数据，但现有数据采集方案难以获得稳定、高频的触觉反馈，限制了其在富接触场景中的效果。

Method: 提出XRoboToolKit-T遥操作系统，包含触觉驱动的力控架构：稳定器触觉模块快速分析法向力分布并推断伪剪切力，实现实时触觉辅助；精炼器触觉模块结合视觉-语言-动作模型，基于触觉传感数据与任务描述预测并优化操作动作。

Result: 在抓取可变形橡胶移液管进行液体转移、将医用注射器插入血管训练垫等富接触任务上验证了有效性，相比无触觉辅助的先进遥操作系统，实现了更高的数据采集效率和更好的操作稳定性。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动的辅助遥操作系统可有效支持高频、稳定的富接触操作与数据采集，拓展了机器人真实技能获取的能力。

Abstract: Collecting high-quality robot data for contact-rich manipulation tasks is essential for enabling robots to acquire real-world skills. However, existing data collection solutions often lack the capability to obtain stable and high-frequency tactile feedback, limiting their effectiveness in contact-rich manipulation scenarios. In this work, we propose a versatile teleoperation system with tactile-driven assistance to enable high-frequency and stable contact-rich manipulation. The proposed XRoboToolKit-T teleoperation system incorporates a tactile-informed force control architecture, designed to ensure both stable and precise force control in contact-rich manipulation during teleoperation. The stabilizer haptic module rapidly analyzes the normal force distribution and infers pseudo shear force, enabling real-time tactile-based assistance during manipulation. The refiner haptic module integrates a vision-language-action model to predict and refine manipulation actions based on tactile sensing data and task descriptions. We apply the proposed teleoperation system to challenging contact-rich manipulation tasks, including grasping a deformable rubber pipette for liquid transfer and inserting a medical syringe into a vascular training pad, to demonstrate the effectiveness of tactile-informed force control. Furthermore, the system achieves higher data collection efficiency and improved manipulation stability compared to state-of-the-art teleoperation without tactile assistance.

</details>


### [281] [The Neverwhere Visual Parkour Benchmark Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16443)
*Ziyu Chen,Henghui Bao,Haoran Chang,Alan Yu,Ran Choi,Kai McClennen,Gio Huh,Kevin Yang,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Yajvan Ravan,John J. Leonard,Xiaolong Wang,Phillip Isola,Ge Yang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出Neverwhere Benchmark Suite，一个由60多个3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建的城市室内外场景组成的超写实闭环评估环境集合，旨在缩小视觉运动控制器的训练/评估差距，并揭示仅依赖3DGS数据训练的潜在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉运动控制器处理复杂视觉环境的能力不断增强，导致在部署前评估其真实世界性能变得越来越困难，训练与评估之间存在较大的差距。作者希望通过构建大规模、可复现、超写实的闭环评估环境来弥合这一差距。

Method: 开发了Neverwhere Benchmark Suite，包含60多个基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）重建的城市室内外场景，使基于高斯泼溅的重建更易于创建并集成到模拟连续测试流程中。同时训练了覆盖多个Neverwhere场景的策略检查点，并在新场景中评估其性能。

Result: 通过在新场景中评估仅基于3DGS生成数据训练的策略检查点，发现其性能存在局限，说明仅依赖3D高斯生成数据进行训练存在潜在缺陷。

Conclusion: 该分析表明，为确保机器人策略的性能，必须采集多样化的数据来源；Neverwhere Benchmark Suite有助于推动大规模、可复现的机器人评估。代码与数据已在项目页面公开。

Abstract: State-of-the-art visual locomotion controllers are increasingly capable at handling complex visual environments, making evaluating their real-world performance before deployment increasingly difficult. This work intends to narrow this train/evaluation gap by developing a collection of hyper-photo-realistic, closed-loop evaluation environments - The Neverwhere Benchmark Suite - comprised of over sixty 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions of urban indoor and outdoor scenes. Our goal is to encourage large-scale and reproducible robot evaluation by making it easier to create and integrate Gaussian splats-based reconstructions into simulated continuous testing setups. We also underscore the potential pitfalls of relying exclusively on 3D Gaussian-generated data for training, by providing policy checkpoints trained over multiple Neverwhere scenes and their performance when evaluated in novel scenes. Our analysis illustrates the necessity of sourcing diverse data to ensure performance. Code and data are available on the project page: https://ziyc.github.io/neverwhere-bench/.

</details>


### [282] [Dense to MoE Adaptation for Compact Vision Language Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16503)
*Muchun Niu,Shuang Chen,Yuzhou Wu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaDE通过将稠密FFN转为MoE并动态屏蔽专家，在停用40%参数下仍保持较高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略参数量不断增长，难以部署到资源受限的机器人平台，需在保持下游任务性能的同时减少部署策略中保留的LLM侧参数。

Method: 将选定的稠密前馈块适配为MoE层，在微调中从路由器统计导出专家保留掩码；Dense2MoE转换在初始化时保持原稠密FFN功能，无需单独恢复阶段；专家掩码依据路由器使用统计动态更新，并采用分阶段训练与专家保护避免早期崩溃。

Result: 在停用40%的LLM参数情况下，AdaDE在LIBERO上保持95.1%平均成功率，在RobotWin2.0全部50项任务上取得42.0%平均成功率。
