Conclusion: 稠密到MoE的适配结合动态专家停用，是缩减活跃VLA模型规模而不造成严重性能损失的一条可行方向。

Abstract: Vision language action (VLA) policies continue to grow in parameter count, making deployment on resource-constrained robot platforms difficult. The central goal is to reduce the number of LLM-side parameters retained in the deployed policy while preserving downstream task performance. Our approach, AdaDE, adapts selected dense feed forward blocks into mixture of experts (MoE) layers and derives expert retention masks from router statistics during fine tuning. The Dense2MoE conversion preserves the original dense FFN function at initialization, so expert deactivation can start without a separate recovery stage. Instead of using a fixed shutdown rule, expert masks are updated dynamically from router usage statistics, with staged training and expert protection to avoid early collapse. With 40% of the LLM parameters deactivated, AdaDE retains 95.1% average success in LIBERO and 42.0% average success across all 50 RobotWin2.0 tasks. These results suggest that dense to MoE adaptation with dynamic expert deactivation is a practical direction for reducing active VLA model size without severe performance loss.

</details>


### [283] [UniDex-ViTac: Learning Unified Visuo-Tactile Dexterous Manipulation Policy from Human Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16504)
*Hyesung Lee,Si-Hwan Heo,Sungwook Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniDex-ViTac 提出用人类视频引导的仿真生成机器人演示与指尖接触观测，训练可部署的视触觉策略，在仿真与真实机器人上均优于纯点云基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频虽能提供灵巧操作演示，但缺乏机器人可执行的动作和触觉测量，难以直接用于训练机器人策略。

Method: 采用物体特定的残差强化学习专家，将标注的人-物交互参考适配到机械臂-手系统；成功轨迹配对最终机器人动作目标与机器人侧指尖接触观测。基于10个物体、50个人类演示，收集10,000条仿真轨迹训练单个 ACT 通用策略，输入为点云、本体感受和四个二进制接触信号（经指尖标签与独立 token 编码），部署时无需人类参考或物体身份与位姿。

Result: 仿真中接触增强配置取得 68.3% 宏观平均成功率，基线为 55.5%；在无真实机器人演示与微调情况下，六个已见和五个未见物体上真实试验成功 73/110（66.4%），基线 60/110（54.5%），提升 11.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持从视频引导的仿真交互中学习统一视触觉灵巧操作策略的可行性。

Abstract: Human videos provide demonstrations of dexterous manipulation but lack robot-executable actions and tactile measurements. We present UniDex-ViTac, a framework that uses human-video-guided simulation to generate robot demonstrations paired with fingertip contact observations for training a deployable visuo-tactile policy. Object-specific residual reinforcement learning specialists adapt annotated human-object interaction references to a robotic arm-hand system. Their successful rollouts pair final robot action targets with robot-side fingertip contact observations. From 50 human demonstrations across ten objects, we collect 10,000 simulated trajectories to train a single Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) based generalist. The policy combines point clouds, proprioception, and four binary contact signals encoded through fingertip labels and a separate token, without requiring human references or privileged object identity and pose at deployment. The contact-augmented configuration achieves 68.3% macro-average success in simulation, compared with 55.5% for the point-cloud-only baseline. Without real-robot demonstrations or policy fine-tuning, it succeeds in 73/110 physical trials (66.4%) across six seen and five unseen objects, compared with 60/110 (54.5%) for the baseline, an increase of 11.8 percentage points. These results support the feasibility of learning a unified visuo-tactile dexterous manipulation policy from video-guided simulated interactions. Project page: https://unidex-vitac.github.io/

</details>


### [284] [ProxiDex: Learning Dynamics-Guided Proximity Policy for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16586)
*Yushan Bai,Boyu Zheng,Zhiyang Mao,Hongzheng Sun,Yuchuang Tong,En Li,Zhengtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ProxiDex，一种动力学引导的手-物接近度策略框架，将手物接近度作为灵巧操作中的交互状态，通过硬件无关的接近线索表征与动作条件动力学建模，提升视觉遮挡和接触不确定性下的操作鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作依赖稳定的手物交互，但该交互在实践中只能被部分观测：视觉常被手部遮挡，触觉传感器带来硬件特定模态与标定负担，且现有策略很少建模这些线索随动作的演化，导致在接触不确定下表现脆弱。

Method: ProxiDex重建交互点云，将几何距离转换为接近线索，形成硬件无关的接触表征，为VR遥操作提供沉浸式反馈；在此基础上，采用耦合的前向-逆向设计学习动作条件接近动力学：由动作预测未来观测隐变量，同时从隐变量变化解码接近度变化；利用该动力学在不同操作阶段自适应重加权接近token，并采用动力学一致性监督引导策略推断。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，ProxiDex在标准物体、未见物体和扰动场景下相较代表性基线方法具有更高的成功率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将手物接近度显式建模为交互状态并引入动作条件动力学，可为灵巧操作提供硬件无关、抗遮挡的稳定策略推断，提升多种场景下的操作性能。

Abstract: Multi-finger dexterous manipulation relies on stable hand-object interactions, yet these interactions are partially observable in practice. Visual observations are often occluded by the hand, tactile sensors introduce hardware-specific modalities and calibration burdens, and existing policies rarely model how these cues evolve under actions, making them brittle under contact uncertainty. To address these, we present ProxiDex, a dynamics-guided proximity policy framework that treats hand-object proximity as an interaction state for dexterous manipulation. ProxiDex reconstructs interaction point clouds and converts geometric distances into proximity cues, forming a hardware-agnostic contact representation that provides immersive feedback during VR teleoperation. Built on this representation, ProxiDex learns action-conditioned proximity dynamics with a coupled forward-inverse design: future observation latents are predicted from actions, while proximity variations are decoded from latent changes. Leveraging these dynamics, ProxiDex adaptively reweights proximity tokens across manipulation phases and uses dynamics-consistency supervision to guide policy inference, stabilizing action generation under unreliable visual feedback. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved success rates and robustness over representative baselines across standard, unseen objects, and perturbation scenarios. Additional visualizations are available at https://proxidex.github.io/.

</details>


### [285] [Learning to Optimize UAV Path Planning for Data Sensing in Wireless Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16629)
*Sijie Ma,Zeyuan Ma,Weijia Cao,Yue-Jiao Gong,Lingling Ma,Zhiyang Huang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Landscape-Aware Meta Differential Evolution（LAMDE），一种学习辅助的规划框架，用于解决WSN数据采集场景中UAV路径规划的两大局限：手工设计偏差和理想化环境假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV路径规划方法存在两点不足：一是主要依赖手工设计，带有设计偏差，难以适应未见任务；二是多通过简化仿真假设理想化空间复杂度，导致实际部署时性能不佳。因此需要一个自适应的学习辅助框架来应对高度约束空间与真实环境的训练数据稀缺、分布偏移问题。

Method: 方法包括：1）重新形式化UAV路径规划问题以纳入挑战性约束；2）提出双层学习优化方法，元层是可训练的算法配置策略，元学习适应性的规划策略供底层规划算法使用；3）引入景观感知的自动增强方案，丰富训练数据以应对数据稀缺与分布偏移；4）底层采用差分进化算法求解；5）设计变长编码策略，动态剪除冗余悬停点并在统一搜索空间中同时优化连续飞行参数。

Result: 基于上述设计元训练LAMDE并与代表性基线对比，综合实验表明LAMDE在WSN数据采集场景下的复杂UAV路径规划任务中取得了state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: LAMDE通过元学习驱动的算法配置、景观感知数据增强以及变长编码差分进化，有效克服了手工设计偏差与环境理想化假设的局限，在复杂UAV路径规划任务中表现优异。

Abstract: UAVs have emerged as highly flexible platforms for data sensing in Wireless Sensor Networks (WSNs). Path planning for UAVs in such tasks plays a key role to assure remote sensing effectiveness and friendly energy consumption. However, existing approaches show two key limitations: i) they are primarily hand-crafted with certain design biases that harm adaptation on unseen tasks. ii) they predominantly assume idealized spatial complexities of actual environments through simplified simulation, causing them to underperform during real-world deployment. In this paper, we propose a novel learning-assisted planning framework, termed Landscape-Aware Meta Differential Evolution (LAMDE), to tackle the mentioned limitations. The major contributions come from the following aspects. We first re-formulate such UAV path planning problem to embrace challenging constraints. To efficiently navigate this highly constrained space, we propose a bi-level learning to optimize approach, where the meta-level is a trainable algorithm configuration policy that meta-learns an adaptable planning strategy for low-level planning algorithm. To address the potential training data scarcity and distribution shift in real-world environments, we introduce a landscape-aware automatic augmentation scheme that enriches training data. At the low-level, a Differential Evolution algorithm is deployed for solving the path planning tasks. To enhance the solving flexibility, we further design a variable-length encoding strategy that dynamically prunes redundant hover points and optimizes continuous flight parameters concurrently within a unified search space. Based on all proposed designs, we meta-train LAMDE and compare it with representative baselines. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that LAMDE achieves state-of-the-art performance on the tested complex UAV path planning tasks in WSN data collection scenarios.

</details>


### [286] [SAVLA: Symmetry-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16641)
*Junle Li,Weixian Waylon Li,Fuxiang Wu,Fusheng Hao,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SAVLA，一种端到端对称感知的VLA模型，通过冻结预训练视觉语言主干、结合等变流匹配动作头与可学习规范化器，实现鲁棒且数据高效的策略学习，在LIBERO上显著提升成功率与旋转鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言动作模型虽为语言条件机器人操作的主流范式，但其空间能力完全来自演示数据，尽管图像与语言本身蕴含几何信息，导致模型仅在演示覆盖的场景位姿范围内可靠。

Method: SAVLA保持预训练视觉语言主干完全冻结，结合等变流匹配动作头与可学习的规范化器。动作头将状态、动作与条件输入分解为不变通道和等变通道，并在所有层中保持该类型划分；规范化器将斜视角图像变换到规范化坐标系，并一致地旋转几何条件。

Result: 在LIBERO上评估，相比GR00T N1.5基线，SAVLA在四个LIBERO套件的平均成功率提升5.1个百分点，并将LIBERO-Goal旋转条件下的平均成功率从41.5%提升至90.4%。

Conclusion: 通过引入对称性感知设计，SAVLA在保持主干冻结的同时有效提升策略的鲁棒性与数据效率，尤其在场景旋转条件下取得大幅改进。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have become the dominant paradigm for language-conditioned robot manipulation. However, although images and language instructions inherently encode geometric information, VLAs acquire their spatial competence purely from demonstrations. As a result, they are reliable only within the range of scene poses that the demonstrations cover. We propose SAVLA, an end-to-end symmetry-aware VLA model for robust and data-efficient policy learning. Our approach keeps the pretrained vision-language backbone entirely frozen while combining it with an equivariant flow-matching action head and a learned canonicalizer. The head decomposes its state, action, and conditioning inputs into invariant and equivariant channels, and preserves this typing throughout all of its layers. The canonicalizer transforms oblique-view images into a canonical frame and rotates the geometric conditions consistently. We evaluate our model on LIBERO. Compared with the GR00T N1.5 baseline, SAVLA improves the success rate averaged over all four LIBERO suites by 5.1 points and increases the mean success rate under rotation on LIBERO-Goal from 41.5% to 90.4%.

</details>


### [287] [WholeBodyWAM: Generalizing Pre-trained World-Action Priors to Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via WBC-Grounded Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16644)
*Zhuo Li,Yiming Yao,Jim Tan,Mengjie Jing,Zhipeng Dong,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WholeBodyWAM，通过联合预测视觉动态、操作动作与全身控制意图，实现可泛化的人形移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界行动模型（WAMs）多聚焦于桌面或手臂操作，人形移动操作（loco-manipulation）探索不足。

Method: 保留预训练的世界-行动先验，同时对异构全身控制器（WBC）语义进行接地，并协调全身行为，联合预测未来视觉动态、操作动作与全身控制意图。

Result: 仿真任务总成功率达91.9%；真实世界分布外任务进度提升0.23；跨WBC成功率方差降低70%。

Conclusion: 通过结构化WBC接地与协调扩展预训练世界-行动先验，而非从头学习全身行为，为可扩展的人形全身智能提供了路径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising approach to general-purpose robot manipulation by jointly modeling visual dynamics and actions. However, most WAM studies focus on tabletop or arm-centric manipulation, while humanoid loco-manipulation remains less explored. To address this gap, we introduce WholeBodyWAM, which jointly predicts future visual dynamics, manipulation actions, and whole-body control intents for generalizable humanoid loco-manipulation. It preserves pre-trained world-action priors while grounding heterogeneous whole-body controller (WBC) semantics and coordinating whole-body behavior. Extensive experiments show that WholeBodyWAM achieves an overall simulation task success rate of 91.9%, with a 0.23 improvement in real-world out-of-distribution task progress and a 70% reduction in success-rate variance across WBCs relative to the respective baselines. These results suggest a path toward scalable humanoid whole-body intelligence by extending pre-trained world-action priors through structured WBC grounding and coordination, rather than relearning whole-body behavior from scratch. Project page: https://wholebodywam.github.io/.

</details>


### [288] [Weave: Learning Whole-Body Dexterous Loco-Manipulation from Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16683)
*Liu Cao,Xingze Wu,Jingzhi Cui,Botian Xu,Mingzhi Pei,Ruoqu Chen,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Weave：从人类演示学习人形机器人全身灵巧物体交互的统一框架，通过接触感知重定向与接近动作补全，训练接触与几何感知策略，联合控制29个身体关节与12个手指关节，9类物体成功率92.5%，未见序列零样本65.0%，并开源约9000次真机rollout约23小时数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人-物体交互需协调全身平衡、行走与灵巧手接触以同时控制机器人与物体运动。人类演示提供了协调交互示例，但跨具身与跨动力学的迁移需学习如何建立并维持有效接触，现有方法难以统一处理该问题。

Method: 提出Weave统一框架：先将采集的人-物交互经接触感知重定向与接近动作补全，转换为可执行的机器人-物体参考轨迹；核心是接触与几何感知策略，联合输出29个身体关节与12个驱动手指关节的控制命令，支持多物体、多交互序列。

Result: 在9类物体上评估：已训练交互成功率92.5%；对训练中未曾见过的序列，无需额外训练达到65.0%成功率。同时发布约9000次物理执行rollout、约23小时数据，含带接触标注的机器人-物体轨迹。

Conclusion: Weave实现了从人类演示到人形机器人全身灵巧交互的可迁移学习，并公开大规模真机交互数据，可支撑下游交互策略学习与物理一致的HOI动作生成。

Abstract: Learning humanoid-object interaction requires coordinating whole-body balance, locomotion, and dexterous hand contact to control both robot and object motion. Human demonstrations provide examples of coordinated interaction, but transferring these behaviors to humanoid robots requires learning how to establish and maintain effective contacts under different embodiments and dynamics. We present Weave, a unified framework for learning whole-body dexterous humanoid-object interaction from captured human demonstrations. Weave first converts captured human-object interactions into executable robot-object references through contact-aware retargeting and approach-motion completion. At its core is a contact- and geometry-aware policy that jointly commands 29 body joints and 12 actuated finger joints across multiple objects and interaction sequences. Evaluation across nine objects yields a 92.5% success rate on trained interactions and, without any additional training, 65.0% on sequences never seen during training. We additionally release ~9,000 physically executed rollouts spanning ~23 hours, providing robot-object trajectories with contact annotations for downstream interaction-policy learning and physically consistent HOI motion generation. Project website: https://xiaohu-art.github.io/Weave/

</details>


### [289] [Differentiable Mesh State Estimation via Factor Graph Inference for Deformable Object Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16686)
*Lidia Al-Zogbi,Fangjie Li,Samuel Tobin,James Ferguson,Nithesh Kumar,Alejandro Chara,Kuan-I Chung,Mingxing Rao,Ayberk Acar,Susheela Sharma Stern,Robert Webster,Daniel Moyer,Alan Kuntz,Caleb Rucker,Tucker Hermans,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因子图的概率化四面体网格状态估计框架，融合物理先验、噪声传感器测量与时序平滑约束，通过非线性最小二乘（LM算法）直接更新可变形物体的网格状态，并在离体气道阻塞实验与形变立方体仿真中验证了其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体状态估计是机器人与仿真领域的核心难题。传统方法难以在统一框架中同时利用物理先验、噪声测量和时序信息，缺乏物理基础扎实且能被测量驱动的网格状态表示。

Method: 以四面体网格作为环境的丰富且具物理意义的表示；在统一的概率建模框架内，将物理先验、含噪传感器测量与时序平滑约束结合为因子图；把估计问题转化为非线性最小二乘优化，并采用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解，直接更新网格节点状态。

Result: 离体中心气道阻塞实验与形变立方体模型仿真表明，方法在刚体运动与变形两种情况下均能实现可靠、准确的重建。

Conclusion: 该概率方法为可变形物体重建中原则化、测量驱动的网格状态估计提供了可行路径，展现出在机器人与仿真场景中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Estimating deformable object states remains a fundamental challenge in robotics and simulation. We propose a novel factor graph-based framework for probabilistic mesh state estimation of deformable objects. The method directly updates a tetrahedral mesh, a rich and physically-grounded representation of an environment, by combining physics priors, noisy sensor measurements, and temporal smoothness constraints within a unified probabilistic formulation. The estimation problem is posed as a nonlinear least-squares optimization and solved using Levenberg-Marquardt. Ex vivo central-airway obstruction experiments and simulations on deforming cube models demonstrate reliable and accurate reconstruction under both rigid motion and deformation, highlighting the potential of this probabilistic approach for principled, measurement-driven mesh state estimation in deformable object reconstruction.

</details>


### [290] [IL-ACT: Imitation Learning with Adaptive Cartesian Tracking Control for a 30-ton Excavator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16696)
*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Seihun Kim,Soyi Jung,Soohyun Park,Jouni Mattila,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IL-ACT运动控制框架，结合模仿学习与自适应笛卡尔跟踪，用于30吨级挖掘机自主控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主挖掘机控制面临耦合运动学、执行器滞后和不确定性等挑战，需提升轨迹跟踪精度与稳定性。

Method: 采用基于操作员示范预训练的14输入模仿策略生成标称关节速率，通过自适应笛卡尔反馈与门控增益/偏置估计进行修正，并由停止距离调节器约束关节参考生成；在Simscape中对100个连续目标及螺旋、8字、圆栅跟踪进行评估。

Result: 相比Teacher+ACT，IL-ACT完成全部目标且耗时更短、终端误差更低；遥测初始化IL-ACT在24项8字和圆栅种子比较中降低RMSE，附加负载螺旋平均RMSE降低约29%，共享传感器噪声下平均RMSE比Teacher+ACT低27.67%，启用估计相比冻结估计降低22.44%。

Conclusion: 分析证明自适应状态与笛卡尔反馈有界，参考可达性取决于调节器可行性；预训练权重效果不一，原教师对比存在螺旋RMSE与最大误差的权衡。

Abstract: Autonomous excavator control is challenged by coupled kinematics, actuation lag, and uncertainty. We propose imitation learning and adaptive Cartesian tracking (IL-ACT), a novel motion control framework for a 30-ton-class excavator. An anchored, 14-input imitation policy pretrained on operator demonstrations generates nominal joint rates; adaptive Cartesian feedback and gated gain/bias estimation correct these commands before a stopping-distance governor constrains joint-reference generation. Simscape evaluation covers 100 sequential goals and spiral, figure-eight, and rounded-raster tracking, including 88 additional runs across three training seeds, two initializations, and speeds, under hydraulic response and sensing conditions. Compared with Teacher+ACT, IL-ACT completes all goals with shorter duration and lower terminal errors under both response conditions. Telemetry-initialized IL-ACT lowers RMSE in all 24 figure-eight and rounded-raster seed comparisons and lowers additional-load spiral mean RMSE by approximately 29%. Original spiral RMSE also improves over IL-only and PID. Under a shared sensor-noise realization, telemetry-initialized IL-ACT achieves 27.67% lower mean RMSE than Teacher+ACT; enabling estimation reduces mean RMSE by $22.44\%$ relative to the frozen estimator. Pretrained-weight effects remain mixed, and the original teacher comparison exhibits a spiral RMSE--maximum-error tradeoff. Analysis establishes bounded adaptive states and Cartesian feedback, with reference admissibility conditional on governor feasibility.

</details>


### [291] [World Models for Embodied Intelligence: From Plausible to Controllable to Actionable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16697)
*Nanjie Yao,Hao Wang,Chong Cheng,Zhikang Chen,Wenzhe Li,Jiafei Lyu,Li Shen,Peilin Zhao,Zongqing Lu,Gao Huang,Steven Hoi,Dacheng Tao,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以"能力层级"而非视觉保真度来评价世界模型的新视角，构建了三级能力框架与3x4矩阵，并据此综述了具身智能中世界模型的研究进展与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的真正价值在于改善行为而非视觉逼真度。人类在抓取杯子前会预判其重量与阻力，这种预判粗糙且非图像化，却能指导行动。然而现有综述多按架构、输出模态或应用领域组织，未能明确回答"哪些预测能力能改善行为"这一核心问题。

Method: 提出三级递进能力框架：1）Plausible模型——保持任务相关的时间、几何或物理结构；2）Controllable模型——进一步预测干预如何改变该结构；3）Actionable模型——将预测转化为规划、行动、学习、评估、验证、恢复或数据选择方面的可量化收益。同时构建3x4矩阵，将几何、物理、动作接地三个维度与以数据、奖励、策略和模型自身为中心的改进循环相交叉。基于该框架综述操作、导航、运动、自动驾驶及通用具身学习领域。

Result: 梳理了各领域的技术演进路径，明确了各层级的 capability 要求，并考察了数据集、基准与评估协议。识别出长时程一致性、不确定性校准、因果干预测试、延迟、验证与恢复、跨具身迁移等关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该视角推动评估标准从视觉合理性转向：预测是否捕捉任务相关状态、是否反映干预效果、是否改善具身智能体的闭环行为。

Abstract: World models connect perception and decision-making in embodied intelligence by maintaining hidden state, anticipating consequences, comparing interventions, and adapting when execution departs from expectations. Although progress is often measured by visual fidelity, their value lies in improving behavior. Before reaching for a cup, a person anticipates its weight and resistance to grasping, shaping the hand before contact. Such anticipation is coarse and rarely pictorial, yet it guides action. This raises a central question: which predictive capabilities improve behavior? Existing surveys, organized by architecture, output modality, or application domain, leave this question implicit. We introduce three progressively stronger capability levels: Plausible models preserve task-relevant temporal, geometric, or physical structure; Controllable models additionally predict how interventions alter that structure; and Actionable models translate predictions into measurable gains in planning, action, learning, evaluation, verification, recovery, or data selection. We complement this hierarchy with a 3 x 4 matrix crossing geometry, physics, and action grounding with improvement loops centered on data, rewards, policies, and the model itself. Using this framework, we survey manipulation, navigation, locomotion, autonomous driving, and general embodied learning, tracing technical progressions, clarifying capability requirements, and examining datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation protocols. We identify challenges in long-horizon consistency, uncertainty calibration, causal intervention testing, latency, verification and recovery, and cross-embodiment transfer. This perspective shifts evaluation from visual plausibility toward whether predictions capture task-relevant state, reflect intervention effects, and improve the closed-loop behavior of embodied agents.

</details>


### [292] [The Robot Data Factory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16705)
*Sami Haddadin,Ivan Laptev,Ian Reid,Dezhen Song,Cesare Stefanini,Abdalla Swikir,Xingxing Zuo,Lyes Saad Saoud,Mahmoud Hamandi,Mohamed Heshmat,Oualid Doukhi,Abdeldjallil Naceri,Attique Bashar,Abdelrahim Mohamed,Teodor Tomic,Yue Peng,Samuel Schneider,Cheng-Chung Lee,Janine Guo,Qinghao Zhang,Kim Jeffery*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Robot Data Factory (RDF)，将机器人数据生成重构为持续的科学生产过程，强调"机器人经验"而非原始数据是Physical AI的核心资源，并构建了从经验形式化到规模化基础设施的完整框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI需要的不只是日益庞大的机器人数据集，智能机器人是通过与物理世界持续交互来获取知识的。因此，作者认为Physical AI的决定性科学资源不是单纯的原始机器人数据，而是"机器人经验"——即保留感知-动作-后果闭环的、有物理基础的交互（包含观测、动作、具身、情境与结果）。

Method: 提出RDF这一任务驱动的基础设施与方法论，用于持续生成、验证、基准测试和复用机器人经验。RDF通过可复现任务、技能课程、同步多模态感知、外部真值、智能体机器网络、数据流水线和动态基准，组织异构机器人与环境专用训练场；实现Deploy-Measure-Learn-Repeat的闭环，服务于世界模型、VLA模型、具身策略、数字孪生及后续部署。同时形式化机器人经验及其质量，提出mission-task-skill-episode-dataset-benchmark-capability层级，并推导连接机器人规模、传感器速率、存储、学习表示、tokenization、训练算力、推理与延迟的量化缩放律及算法合成流程。

Result: 框架在面向家庭、环境与能源应用的三个互补物理训练场中得到实例化。量化缩放律与算法合成流程将机器人队伍规模、传感器速率、存储、学习表示、tokenization、训练算力、推理与延迟同Embodied-AI集群需求关联起来。

Conclusion: RDF将机器人数据生成重构为持续的科学生产过程，为Physical AI提供了通向可复现、可扩展乃至最终联邦化基础设施的路径。

Abstract: Physical AI requires more than increasingly large robot datasets: intelligent robots acquire knowledge through continuous interaction with the physical world. We argue that the defining scientific resource of Physical AI is therefore not raw robot data alone, but robot experience - physically grounded interaction whose observations, actions, embodiment, context, and outcomes preserve the perception-action-consequence loop. We introduce the Robot Data Factory (RDF), a mission-driven infrastructure and methodology for continuously generating, validating, benchmarking, and reusing such experience. RDF organizes heterogeneous robots and environment-specific training grounds through reproducible missions, skill curricula, synchronized multimodal sensing, external ground truth, an agentic robot network, data pipelines, and living benchmarks. Rather than treating datasets as static end products, RDF implements a closed Deploy-Measure-Learn-Repeat cycle in which validated physical experience supports world models, vision-language-action models, embodied policies, digital twins, and subsequent robot deployment. We further formalize robot experience and its quality, introduce a mission-task-skill-episode-dataset-benchmark-capability hierarchy, and derive quantitative scaling laws and an algorithmic synthesis procedure connecting robot fleet size, sensor rates, storage, learning representations, tokenization, training compute, inference, and latency to Embodied-AI cluster requirements. The framework is instantiated in three complementary physical training grounds for domestic, environmental, and energy applications. RDF thus reframes robot data generation as a continuous scientific production process and provides a pathway toward reproducible, scalable, and eventually federated infrastructure for Physical AI.

</details>


### [293] [CorrRisk-WM: Corridor-Conditioned Risk World Modeling for Safety-Critical Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16724)
*Tingyu Guo,Reza Langari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CorrRisk-WM，一种面向规划的部分世界模型，将环境演化与候选轨迹走廊上的入侵/近失事件预测相耦合，在Waymo大规模验证集上取得高精度风险预测与更低碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全局部规划需要预测周围智能体运动并评估"候选轨迹特定"的风险，因为相同的智能体运动对不同自车轨迹可能意味着不同风险，传统方法难以捕捉这种候选条件化的风险差异。

Method: 构建部分世界模型：潜环境模型递归预测智能体状态并更新智能体-智能体、智能体-地图交互；每个候选轨迹通过足迹感知几何与学习到的智能体-走廊表示查询演化环境；轻量循环风险模块利用时序上下文估计逐切片危险，经生存聚合得到首次进入与时间跨度级事件概率。

Result: 在100个Waymo验证分片的29,176个场景上，入侵AP达0.8567，1米近失首次进入AP达0.8671；基线对比中在三个距离阈值下近失AP最高，开环碰撞率最低（4.88%），路径进展15.35米。消融实验显示移除动态环境建模或候选条件几何交互，平均入侵AP从0.8590分别降至0.7624与0.7252。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将环境演化与候选条件化几何推理相耦合，对风险预测和安全导向的候选轨迹选择具有显著支撑作用。

Abstract: Safe local planning requires forecasting surrounding-agent motion and evaluating candidate-specific risks, since identical agent motion can pose different risks to different ego trajectories. We present CorrRisk-WM, a planning-oriented partial world model coupling environment evolution with supervised intrusion and near-miss prediction over bounded candidate-trajectory corridors. A latent environment model recursively predicts agent states and updates agent-agent and agent-map interactions. Each candidate queries the evolving environment through footprint- aware geometry and learned agent-corridor representations. A lightweight recurrent risk module uses temporal context to estimate per-slice hazards; survival aggregation yields first-entry and horizon-level event probabilities. On 29,176 scenarios from 100 Waymo validation shards, CorrRisk-WM achieves intrusion average precision (AP) of 0.8567 and 1-m near-miss first-entry AP of 0.8671. In baseline comparisons, it attains the highest near-miss AP at all three distance thresholds and the lowest observed open-loop collision rate (4.88%), with route progress of 15.35 m. Across three seeds, removing dynamic environment modeling or candidate-conditioned geometric interaction reduces mean intrusion AP from 0.8590 to 0.7624 and 0.7252, respectively. These results support coupling environment evolution with candidate-conditioned geometric reasoning for risk prediction and safety-oriented candidate selection.

</details>


### [294] [Seeing What Matters: Visual Cue Guided Video Planning for Generalizable Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16737)
*Hojin Lee,Sizhe Lester Li,Maximilian Hilger,Susie Lu,Achim J. Lilienthal,Vincent Sitzmann,Daniel A. Duecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CueNav是一个基于视频模型的导航框架，通过视觉线索引导视频规划并结合具身特定逆动力学模型，实现长时程规划与精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频规划方法多依赖短时程引导，并通过场景重建恢复几何路点，长时程规划和精确的视频到动作转换仍未被充分探索。

Method: 使用鸟瞰图（BEV）地图传递全局任务上下文，并在自中心观测中保留部分机器人本体以暴露具身上下文作为视觉线索引导视频规划器；同时用逆动力学模型（IDM）将视频计划中的稠密流场转换为机器人动作。

Result: 在迷宫导航中成功率比无线索规划提升近2倍；在狭窄通道中达到70%成功率；并展示零样本语义条件导航及同一视频规划器跨不同机器人平台部署。

Conclusion: 视觉线索引导的视频规划结合具身特定动作接地，为可泛化的长时程规划与具身感知控制导航框架提供了路径。

Abstract: Generative video models can serve as a promising backbone for robot navigation by predicting future observations as video plans. Recent approaches often condition video planning on short-horizon guidance and recover geometric waypoints through scene reconstruction, leaving longer-horizon planning and precise video-to-action translation less explored. We present CueNav, a video model-based navigation framework combining visual cue guided video planning with an embodiment-specific Inverse-Dynamics Model (IDM). As visual cues, we use a Bird's-Eye View (BEV) map to convey global task context and retain part of the robot body in the egocentric observation to expose embodiment context. These cues guide the video planner, while the IDM translates dense flow fields extracted from the video plan into robot actions. With the visual cue encoding global task context, CueNav achieves nearly 2x higher success in maze navigation than planning without the cue. The body-aware view with the IDM enables precise navigation with 70% success in a narrow passage where comparison methods largely fail to complete the task. We further demonstrate zero-shot semantic-conditioned navigation and deployment of the same video planner across different robot platforms. Our results show that visual cue-guided video planning with embodiment-specific action grounding paves the way toward a generalizable navigation framework for longer-horizon planning and embodiment-aware control. Additional results and code are available on our project website: https://cuenav.github.io.

</details>


### [295] [The Latent That Never Was: A Forensic Re-run of the CVAE Ablation in Action Chunking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16745)
*Bo Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 复现ACT原始消融实验，未能重现编码器移除导致成功率从35%降至2%的结果，差异可能源于训练时长与检查点选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACT广泛用于从演示中学习机器人操作，原论文称移除条件VAE编码器会使成功率从35%降到2%，作者希望验证该结论是否依赖具体实现或训练数据。

Method: 在原始代码中重跑该消融实验，并改变训练时长和用于评估的检查点选择方式；同时分析潜变量对动作重建的贡献，并在推理时考察潜变量使用情况，以及计时跳过编码器后的训练吞吐。

Result: 已发表的性能骤降未能重现，成功率仅有不确定的小幅增减；训练时长和检查点选择都能反转哪个策略得分更高，但原骤降原因仍不明。在所测ACT基准上，采样潜变量在各非零潜信息惩罚权重下对重建几乎无帮助，推理时ACT将潜变量置零不使用；跳过编码器可提高两种实现的训练吞吐。

Conclusion: 原论文的编码器重要结论缺乏可复现证据，其作用需在其他任务上进一步检验；作者公开代码、评估工具和结果以便重复比较。

Abstract: Action Chunking Transformers (ACT) are widely used to learn robot manipulation from demonstrations. Their conditional variational autoencoder includes an encoder meant to capture differences between demonstrations during training. The original ACT paper reported that encoder removal dropped the mean success rate from 35% to 2% on two simulated tasks with human demonstrations. We re-ran this ablation in the original code and checked whether the findings depend on the implementation or training data. The published drop does not reappear in our tests, although smaller gains or losses in success rate remain uncertain. To investigate the discrepancy, we varied training length and how checkpoints are selected for evaluation. Both can reverse which policy scores higher, but the published drop's cause remains unknown. Success rates alone leave open whether the encoder provides information that helps the policy reconstruct demonstrated actions. On the tested ACT benchmark, the sampled latent provides little reconstruction benefit at every tested nonzero weight of the penalty on latent information. At inference, ACT leaves this latent unused and sets it to zero. Skipping the encoder increases training throughput in both implementations we timed. We release code, evaluation tools and results so others can repeat the comparisons and test the encoder on other tasks.

</details>


### [296] [Optimal Excitation Trajectories for System Identification of Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16786)
*Fotis Panetsos,Kostas J. Kyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向水下航行器系统辨识的结构化方法，通过Bezier曲线参数化最优激励轨迹，并在实验水池中验证了辨识参数的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器动力学参数辨识依赖高质量数据，但受限于运动安全约束，需要设计既动态可行又能最大化信息量的激励轨迹，以提升参数估计的可靠性。

Method: 采用Bezier曲线对轨迹进行参数化，保证运动曲线光滑可微，并通过控制点操作方便地施加约束；构建优化问题，在满足安全限制的前提下寻找动态可行的激励轨迹，以最大化采集数据质量，随后利用最小二乘法估计航行器动态参数。

Result: 在实验室水槽中进行实验验证，用优化轨迹辨识得到的动态参数，通过在未见轨迹上前向仿真预测航行器速度来进行评估。

Conclusion: 该结构化方法能够生成安全且信息丰富的激励轨迹，支持可靠的参数辨识，并通过预测未见过轨迹上的速度验证了辨识模型的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a structured methodology for the system identification of underwater vehicles through the design of optimal excitation trajectories. To this end, the trajectories are parameterized using Bezier curves, which ensure smooth and differentiable motion profiles while facilitating the enforcement of constraints through appropriate manipulation of the control points. An optimization problem is formulated to determine a dynamically feasible excitation trajectory that respects safety limits and maximizes the quality of the collected data, thereby enabling reliable estimation of the vehicle's dynamic parameters using least squares. The proposed methodology is experimentally validated in a laboratory water tank, where the dynamic parameters, identified from the optimized trajectory, are evaluated by predicting the vehicle's velocity through forward simulation on previously unseen trajectories.

</details>


### [297] [Motion planning in high dimensional spaces hybridizing RRT and HAR via position-direction decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16810)
*Frederic Cazals,Nelson Feyeux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合RRT与Hit-and-Run随机游走的采样路径规划方法，通过解耦扩展点与扩展方向，并引入稀疏移动策略，可高效求解高达64个机器人、384自由度的高维规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间路径规划在存在狭窄通道和小间隙时非常困难，传统方法难以应对复杂的多机器人或分子系统场景。

Method: 提出基于采样的规划方法，将RRT与Hit-and-Run随机游走结合，把待扩展点与扩展方向解耦；证明RRT与HAR是该通用算法的特例（分别对应不同的点与方向偏置耦合）；并引入稀疏移动策略，每步仅移动比例为p_r的机器人。

Result: 在3D经典钢琴搬运问题和涉及数十个刚体域的复杂分子系统（建模为SE(3)^N运动空间中的独立机器人）上测试，标准笔记本数秒内可求解多达64个机器人、384自由度的问题；HARF方法比每步移动全部机器人的经典RRT快达两个数量级，在多数实例中即使都用最优p_r也快达2.4倍。

Conclusion: 推荐HARF作为复杂多机器人规划问题的首选方法，因其在高维复杂场景中兼具速度与成功率优势。

Abstract: The exploration of high-dimensional spaces remains a challenging problem, in particular in the presence of narrow passages and small clearances. We propose novel sampling-based path-planning methods for high-dimensional spaces combining Rapidly-exploring Random Trees (RRT) and Hit-and-Run (HAR) random walks by decoupling the point being extended from the direction of extension. We also show that RRT and HAR appear as special cases of a generic algorithm coupling the biases used for the point and direction extension, respectively. We further study a sparse-move strategy in which only a fraction p_r of the robots is moved at each step, helping both RRT and the proposed HAR algorithms handle cluttered instances. Tests are presented for two families of models: classical piano mover problems in 3D, and complex molecular systems involving tens of rigid domains moving relatively to one another -- the latter viewed as independent robots exploring the motion space SE(3)N . Within seconds on a standard laptop, our algorithms solve instances with up to 64 robots and 384 degrees of freedom. We conclude by suggesting one of our methods, HARF, as the method of choice for complex multi-robot planning problems, being up to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical RRT moving all robots at each step--when it succeeds at all, and still up to 2.4 fold faster on most instances when both use their best p_r.

</details>


### [298] [Rethinking Visual Embodiment Dependence in Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16815)
*Hongjie Fang,Yuxuan Lu,Chenxi Wang,Haoxiang Qin,Shirun Tang,Zihao He,Shangning Xia,Jingjing Chen,Wanxi Liu,Shiquan Wang,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"视觉具身依赖"(VED)概念，并通过对3D点云进行具身规范化来结构化管理视觉具身信息，提升人到机器人的策略迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略同时观察任务场景和执行具身，导致具身特定的视觉线索可能成为动作预测的"捷径"，影响策略对任务进度的判断与泛化能力。作者主张不应简单消除VED，而应围绕支持控制与泛化的具身信息对其进行结构化组织。

Method: 通过跨多种代表性策略的线索冲突干预实验揭示VED现象；在3D点云中实现具身规范化，用规范末端执行器表示(CER)替换原始具身，保留控制相关几何同时抽象具身特定形态；利用CER的可编辑性设计配置去相关增强，应对陌生机器人配置。

Result: 具身规范化显著提升无机器人演示下的人到机器人策略迁移；仅移除具身而不保留控制相关几何则效果不足；CER本身在机器人配置与任务进度解耦时可能成为配置捷径，配置去相关增强可缓解该失效模式并恢复鲁棒恢复能力，且不损失已见配置上的性能。

Conclusion: 健壮的视觉运动学习受益于对视觉具身信息进行结构化组织而非简单移除。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies observe both the task scene and the acting embodiment, allowing embodiment-specific visual cues to influence action prediction. We study this phenomenon as visual embodiment dependence (VED) and show, through cue-conflict interventions across representative policies, that visible robot configuration can become a shortcut to task progress. Rather than eliminating VED, we argue that it should be structured around embodiment information that supports control and generalization. We realize this through embodiment canonicalization in 3D point clouds, replacing the original embodiment with a canonical end-effector representation (CER) that preserves control-relevant geometry while abstracting embodiment-specific morphology. Its editable form further enables configuration-decorrelation augmentation for unfamiliar robot configurations. Experiments show that embodiment canonicalization substantially improves human-to-robot policy transfer without robot demonstrations, while simply removing the embodiment is insufficient without preserving control-relevant geometry. We further find that CER itself can become a configuration shortcut when robot configuration becomes decoupled from task progress; configuration-decorrelation augmentation mitigates this failure mode and restores robust recovery without sacrificing performance on seen configurations. Together, these results show that robust visuomotor learning benefits from structuring, rather than removing, visual embodiment information. Project website: https://tonyfang.net/ved

</details>


### [299] [TEMPO: Learning Temporal Context for Dynamic Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16864)
*Zhenyang Feng,Jimin Heo,Erik B. Sudderth,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出单帧观测的VLA模型在动态操作中因"运动歧义"和"状态混叠"两类表征缺陷而受限，非模型规模问题；提出TEMPO，通过引入运动摘要与本体感受历史两种时序输入解决，无需改动骨干网络，显著提升动态操作成功率并发布TEMPO-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在准静态操作中表现优异，但推理时仅依赖单帧观测，难以应对动态操作任务。作者识别出两个根本性表征缺陷：一是运动歧义（单帧观测缺乏场景动态信息，无法预测运动物体的未来状态），二是状态混叠（任务不同阶段视觉相似的观测需要不同动作）。作者论证这些缺陷与模型规模和推理延迟无关，瓶颈在于缺失的时序上下文而非模型容量。

Method: 提出TEMPO，为预训练VLA增加两种时序输入：一是从冻结的视频基础模型中提取的运动摘要，用于解决运动歧义；二是紧凑的本体感受历史，用于解决状态混叠。该方法无需修改骨干网络，训练和部署时的额外计算开销极小。

Result: 在四项动态操作任务中，TEMPO将Bottle Handover成功率从44%提升至74%，并且是唯一能解决状态混叠的方法。探测实验与消融研究证实，每种时序信号分别独立地解决其对应的表征缺陷。

Conclusion: 单帧观测的时序上下文缺失是VLA模型动态操作能力受限的关键瓶颈，通过引入运动摘要与本体感受历史能够有效弥补该缺陷。作者还发布了TEMPO-Bench，包含超过5万帧标注数据，用于以回归和多项选择两种形式评估运动感知的机器人感知能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved impressive performance in quasi-static manipulation, but struggle in dynamic manipulation tasks because they operate on a single observation at inference time. We identify two representational failures that underlie this limitation. The first is motion ambiguity, where a single observation does not include scene dynamics and therefore cannot anticipate the future state of moving objects. The second is state aliasing, where visually similar observations from different points in a task require different actions. We argue that these failures persist regardless of model scale and inference latency, showing that the bottleneck is missing temporal context rather than model capacity. Based on this insight, we propose TEMPO, which augments a pretrained VLA with two temporal inputs: a motion summary extracted from a frozen video foundation model to resolve motion ambiguity and a compact proprioceptive history to resolve state aliasing. TEMPO requires no modification to the backbone and adds minimal compute overhead at training or deployment. Across four dynamic manipulation tasks, it improves Bottle Handover success from 44% to 74% and is the only method that solves state aliasing. Probing and ablation studies confirm that each temporal signal independently addresses its corresponding failure. We further release TEMPO-Bench, a benchmark of over 50k annotated frames for evaluating motion-aware robot perception in both regression and multiple-choice formats.
  Project Website: https://tempo-robot.github.io/

</details>


### [300] [Artificial Intelligence-Enabled Space Robot Operations: Technologies, Challenges and Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16880)
*Zeyuan Huang,Gang Chen,Zixuan Hao,Guoqin Tang,Junyi Zong,Guoyou Ban,Jiale Wang,Haoyang Lv,Chaoqian Ren,Sitong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述从"能力构建"视角系统梳理了人工智能赋能空间机器人操作（AI-SRO）的研究，提出三层技术框架，并展望关键研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间机器人需在有限人工干预下完成长周期、富接触、多阶段操作，AI与具身基础模型带来新机遇，但受任务数据稀缺、空间特有动力学与感知条件、星上资源受限及严苛安全要求制约，其向空间迁移面临挑战。

Method: 采用综述方法：先总结典型操作场景、自主化趋势与空间特有约束；再建立包含能力基础、能力形成、能力部署与演化三个层次的技术框架，并在此框架内评述仿真环境/数据集/基准、任务与环境理解/状态感知/决策规划/动作执行，以及星上部署/地空适应/持续学习/能力迁移等工作。

Result: 构建了AI-SRO的三层能力框架，系统梳理了各层次代表性研究，明确了现有仿真、数据、感知、决策与部署环节的研究现状与不足。

Conclusion: 提出面向可信仿真与数据、开放世界多模态认知、长时程安全决策、物理约束下的策略学习以及空间计算基础设施等关键研究方向。

Abstract: Space robots are increasingly expected to perform long-duration, contact-rich, and multi-stage operations with limited human intervention. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), robot learning, and embodied foundation models provide new opportunities to improve the autonomy and adaptability of such systems, but their transfer to space is constrained by scarce mission data, space-specific dynamics and sensing conditions, limited onboard resources, and stringent safety requirements. This article reviews artificial intelligence-enabled space robot operations (AI-SRO) from a capability-building perspective. We first summarize representative operational scenarios, autonomy trends, and space-specific constraints. We then establish a three-layer technical framework comprising capability foundations, capability formation, and capability deployment/evolution. Within this framework, we review simulation environments, datasets and benchmarks; task and environment understanding, state perception, decision-making and planning, and action execution; and onboard deployment, ground-to-space adaptation, continual learning, and capability transfer. Finally, we propose key research directions toward trustworthy simulation and data, open-world multimodal cognition, long-horizon safe decision-making, physically constrained policy learning, and space computing infrastructures.

</details>


### [301] [Waggle Dance Inspired Motion Communication for Multiple UAVs in MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16958)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受蜜蜂摇摆舞启发的MuJoCo通信系统，实现单个表演者向多个观察者广播导航信息，并验证了有限条件下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜜蜂摇摆舞中一个个体的运动可传递空间信息并引导其他个体行动。作者希望将现有MoCom的点对点运动通信扩展为一对多的广播式通信机制。

Method: 在MuJoCo中构建一个表演者和一至五个观察者的系统。表演者用四个飞行动作原语和显式空信号广播六位导航载荷；每个观察者独立处理自身RGB图像，提取光流轨迹、识别符号、解析信息，并在确认完整帧后才启动导航。接收状态与执行触发在各观察者间相互独立，仿真控制与安全检查共享真值。

Result: 在静止观察者、25 Hz图像输入和理想状态反馈控制下，标准测试集17次标称广播共53次接收暴露，得到44条正确完整消息，13次广播通过组级解码与执行检查；3个无消息或输入故障对照符合预期。补充测试集在同一冻结代码和默认几何下3次广播完成14次成功接收暴露。近距和广角配置暴露了跟踪与识别失败，未成功接收者保持静止。

Conclusion: 有限的仿真结果支持在测试条件下实现受摇摆舞启发的“广播到行动”机制的可行性，同时指出了当前感知与协议方面的限制。

Abstract: The honeybee waggle dance motivates a communication mechanism in which one agent's movement conveys spatial information that guides other agents' actions. This paper presents a MuJoCo system that extends the point-to-point motion communication setting of MoCom to one performer and multiple observers. A performer broadcasts a six-bit navigation payload using four flight primitives and explicit null signals. Each of one to five observers processes its own onboard RGB images, extracts optical-flow trajectories, recognizes symbols, parses the message, and starts navigation only after confirming its own complete frame. Reception states and execution triggers are separate across observers, while simulation control and safety checks use shared ground truth. With stationary observers, 25 Hz image input, and ideal state-feedback control, a fixed standard suite yielded 44 correct complete messages from 53 receiver exposures across 17 nominal broadcasts; 13 broadcasts passed all group-level decoding and execution checks. Three additional no-message or input-fault controls met their expected outcomes. A separately reported supplemental suite, using the same frozen code at the default geometry, achieved 14 successful receiver exposures across three broadcasts. Near-range and wide-angle configurations exposed tracking and recognition failures, while unsuccessful receivers remained stationary. These finite simulation results support the feasibility of a waggle-dance-inspired broadcast-to-action mechanism under the tested conditions and identify the present perceptual and protocol limits.

</details>


### [302] [Overcoming technical adoption barriers for mobile service robots in rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16996)
*Christian Sternitzke,Sebastian Blumenthal,Lukas Kleedoerfer,Verena Deserno,Anke Mayfarth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文阐述医疗移动服务机器人如何通过外围功能与渐进改进从早期技术成熟度提升至较高水平，强调此类下游开发对用户接受度与临床推广的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗机器人研究多停留在低技术成熟度的早期阶段，而系统真正被临床接受所需的功能改进往往无法通过事前询问用户获得，只能在临床或实际运营环境测试中才能发现。

Method: 以一台已获批为医疗设备的移动服务机器人为案例，通过增加外围功能和进行小幅改进来提升其技术成熟度，并总结该过程中的经验。

Result: 外围功能与渐进式改进对提升技术成熟度和用户接受度具有关键作用，这类改进通常只有在临床或运营环境中实测才能被识别。

Conclusion: 建议医疗服务机器人开发者提前规划这类下游开发工作，因其可能耗时较长，但却是获得用户认可、实现系统广泛应用的必经之路。

Abstract: Many publications on robotic systems in healthcare describe early-stage work on low technology readiness levels. This paper describes how a mobile service robot approved as a medical device reaches higher technology readiness levels by adding peripheral functions and smaller improvements, which are pivotal for user acceptance in clinical environments and which often cannot be elicited by questioning users ex-ante as certain aspects only come in mind from testing the systems in clinical settings or operational environments. Especially developers of service robots in healthcare are advised to plan with such downstream developments, which can take significant implementation time, to obtain user acceptance and achieve widespread adoption of their robotic systems.

</details>


### [303] [SWIM: Vision-Language-Grounded Soft Whole-Body Interactive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17035)
*Tingcong Liu,Aye Phyu Phyu Aung,Junjie Xiong,Siyi Ma,Bo An,Ke Wu,Senthilnath Jayavelu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SWIM框架将RGB观测与语言指令映射为完整驱动指令序列，通过扩散动作头与视觉软本体感知（VSP）共享表征，结合迭代虚拟推演与内在柔顺实现软体机器人的全身操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体与连续体机器人依赖分布式本体变形与接触实现操控，但如何将语言和视觉语境转化为可执行的全身驱动仍是根本性难题。现有VLA方法难以在有限示教下保留本体几何信息，且在线策略查询成本高。

Method: 提出SWIM框架：其VLA策略SWIM-VLA将扩散动作头与视觉软本体感知（VSP）结合，通过RGB观测、语言指令与肌腱状态的共享表征建模专家指令块的条件分布；VSP用仿真真值监督有序体锚点预测；再由具身机械智能通过不断演化的仿真观测迭代虚拟推演生成指令序列，内在柔顺性提供局部接触自适应而无需在线查询策略。

Result: 在平面肌腱驱动软体机器人上评估打包、到达与抓取任务（抓取目标锚定）。仿真中SWIM-VLA成功率分别为100%、96%、88%，优于适配的OpenVLA-OFT基线及受控消融；硬件上为100%、80%、75%，而直接在线部署同一策略检查点为75%、40%、25%。

Conclusion: SWIM通过共享表征、扩散策略与本体感知监督，并结合迭代虚拟推演与机械柔顺，有效实现了软体机器人的语言-视觉-全身驱动控制，仿真与真实硬件上均显著优于直接在线部署基线。

Abstract: Soft and continuum robots enable manipulation through distributed body deformation and contact, yet translating language and visual context into executable whole-body actuation remains a fundamental challenge. We present SWIM, a framework that maps an initial RGB observation and a language instruction to a complete actuation-command sequence. Its vision-language-action (VLA) policy, SWIM-VLA, combines a diffusion action head with Visual Soft Proprioception (VSP) through a shared representation of RGB observations, language instructions, and tendon states. The diffusion head models conditional distributions of expert command chunks, while VSP supervises ordered body-anchor predictions using simulation ground truth, encouraging the representation to retain body geometry when learning from limited demonstrations. Embodied mechanical intelligence supports physical execution of command sequences generated through iterative virtual rollout from evolving simulated observations, with intrinsic compliance providing local contact adaptation without online policy queries. We evaluate SWIM on packing, reaching, and grasping on a planar tendon-driven soft robot, with grasping targets anchored. In simulation, SWIM-VLA achieves success rates of 100\%, 96\%, and 88\%, respectively, outperforming an adapted OpenVLA-OFT baseline and controlled ablations. On hardware, SWIM achieves success rates of 100\%, 80\%, and 75\%, compared with 75\%, 40\%, and 25\% for direct online deployment of the same policy checkpoint.

</details>


### [304] [BRAVE-6D: Benchmark for Robotic Active Vision in 6DOF Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17106)
*Philipp Ausserlechner,Bernhard Neuberger,Alessandro Scherl,Michael Schebek,Stefan Thalhammer,Markus Vincze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BRAVE-6D基准，利用3D高斯溅射（3DGS）的视图合成技术，为机器人主动视觉系统在物体位姿估计任务中的评测提供统一场景与工具，并给出在场景内进行视觉伺服并准确估计小物体位姿的基线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测与抓取小物体在机器人领域仍是重大挑战。主动视觉（让机器人靠近物体）是直观的解决思路，但不同方法难以在相同物理场景设置下公平比较，因此需要一个统一基准。

Method: 提出BRAVE-6D基准，基于高斯溅射（3DGS）的视图合成，为主动视觉系统提供可用于评测的场景与工具，用于物体位姿估计这一抓取前的关键步骤。

Result: 展示了基线方案：在场景内执行视觉伺服，并能准确估计小物体的位姿。

Conclusion: BRAVE-6D为机器人主动视觉与物体位姿估计提供了可复现、可比较的评测平台，验证了基于3DGS视图合成的基准构建与基线方法的可行性。

Abstract: Detecting and grasping small objects remains a significant challenge in robotics. Active vision, where the robot moves closer to the object, is an intuitive solution, yet comparing approaches on common ground is difficult since identical physical scene setups are required. Hence, we introduce BRAVE-6D, a benchmark designed to evaluate robotic active vision systems for object pose estimation, a crucial first step in grasping objects. BRAVE-6D leverages view synthesis based on Gaussian Splats (3DGS) to provide scenes and tools for benchmarking active vision systems. We show baseline solutions performing visual servoing within the scene and accurately estimating the poses of small objects.

</details>


### [305] [Intrinsic Robot Rewarding: Reusing VLA Representations for Autonomous Evaluation and Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17115)
*Tobias Schaffer,Mohab Elkhayat,Daniela Nicklas,Mustafa Almohamad,Elham Al-Fuqara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"内在机器人奖励"(IRR)：复用VLA系统已有的视觉表征与成功示范，构建奖励机制用于机器人结果评估与策略改进，无需额外评价器或感知骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA系统已具备丰富视觉表征与成功示范两大资源，但仅用于任务执行。作者希望将这些既有资源二次利用于机器人自身结果的评估与策略改进，以降低集成成本、提高奖励计算效率并减少人工打分。

Method: 以成功示范的终点作为任务特定参考，以策略冻结的视觉编码器作为特征空间，在现有流程中加入参考库与打分操作，实现奖励机制，不依赖独立学习的评价器或额外感知骨干。

Result: 给出了奖励公式、核心研究问题与评估方法（将奖励可靠性与任务成功率、监督投入挂钩）；已具备COMAU Racer 3演示平台，达到TRL 4。

Conclusion: IRR将视觉奖励与经验学习思路融入机器人既有感知与示范流程，当前为实验室基础，下一步是把内部结果评估接入物理策略改进，目标是形成可复用、能利用工业机器人既有数据与经验的学习方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) systems already bring together two valuable resources for robot learning: rich visual representations and demonstrations of successful task execution. Intrinsic Robot Rewarding (IRR) proposes to use these resources for a second, complementary purpose: evaluating the robot's own outcomes and providing feedback for policy improvement. Successful demonstration endpoints define task-specific references, and the policy's frozen visual encoder provides the feature space in which new outcomes are assessed. The core reward mechanism adds a reference bank and a scoring operation to the existing pipeline, without requiring a separate learned evaluator or an additional perception backbone. Our position is that this reuse offers a promising route to lower integration effort, efficient reward computation, and reduced recurring human outcome scoring. Building on established research in visual rewards and learning from experience, IRR brings these ideas into the robot's existing perception and demonstration pipeline. An operational COMAU Racer 3 demonstrator is available at technology readiness level 4 (TRL 4). This laboratory foundation supports the next research step: connecting internal outcome evaluation to physical policy improvement. We present the reward formulation, central research questions, and an evaluation methodology linking reward reliability to task success and supervision effort. The intended contribution is a reusable approach to learn and improve from the data and experience already available in industrial robot systems.

</details>


### [306] [LOTUSim-Energy: A Maritime Simulator for Human-Drone Interaction in Autonomous Offshore Operation \&amp; Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17124)
*Juliette Grosset,Marie Dubromel,Hélène Lechêne,Quentin Arzel,Cédric Buche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LOTUSim-Energy，一个面向海上运维多域人机交互的实时海事仿真平台，统一异构无人载具、环境力耦合与沉浸式监控界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上运维需在空中、水面与水下面跨域作业并依赖人工监督，现有仿真工具难以统一异构载具、环境扰动与人类监督，导致海上部署风险高。

Method: 采用分布式架构耦合风、浪、流等环境力，在统一平台中集成无人机、无人水面艇、自主水下航行器与遥控潜水器；提供桌面与虚拟现实沉浸式监督界面；构建结构化海上任务库以支持自主性栈的可重复评估；模块化集成真实物理、能量感知电池建模与故障检测流程。

Result: 在单桩与过渡段结构的多域巡检场景中验证系统级性能，评估集成航点跟随插件与 AIS 参考轨迹跟踪在实时能量监测下的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过融合统一环境物理、异构载具仿真与沉浸式监督，LOTUSim-Energy 为海上人机协作工作流的原型设计与预演提供集成测试床，有助于降低海上部署风险。

Abstract: Offshore maintenance requires operations in the air, the surface, and the subsea domain and include human supervision. This paper presents LOTUSim-Energy, a real-time maritime simulator designed for multi-domain human--drone interaction for offshore operation and maintenance. The plat- form unifies heterogeneous unmanned vehicles (Unmanned Aerial Vehicles: UAVs, Unmanned Surface Vehicles: USVs, Autonomous Underwater Vehicles: AUVs, Remotely Operated Vehicles: ROVs) within a distributed architecture coupling environment forcing (wind, waves, currents) and provides immersive user interfaces for supervision (desktop and virtual reality). A structured offshore task library enables repeatable evaluation of autonomy stacks under realistic metocean disturbances. The simulator supports realistic physics, energy-aware battery modeling, and fault-detection pipelines as modular validation tools. System-level performance is demonstrated on a multi-domain inspection scenario for monopile and transition piece structure, where we evaluate the reliability of integrated waypoint-follower plugin and Automatic Identification System (AIS)-referenced trajectory tracking under real-time energy monitoring. By combining unified environmental physics, heterogeneous vehicle simulation, and immersive supervision, LOTUSim-Energy provides an integration testbed for prototyping and rehearsing offshore human--robot collaboration workflows, as a step toward de-risking sea deployment.

</details>


### [307] [Continual Learning for Traversability Prediction with Uncertainty-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17141)
*Hojin Lee,Yunho Lee,Daniel A Duecker,Cheolhyeon Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向非结构化环境自主导航的持续学习框架，用于地形可通行性预测，通过生成式经验回放模型实现对新地形的增量适应，无需存储历史数据，并能结合生成样本的不确定性实现不确定性感知的适应，从而缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，机器人-地形交互复杂且不确定，易导致牵引力丧失和动态失稳，可通行性预测至关重要。现有基于学习的方法往往难以适应新地形；即便实现适应，也难以保留此前环境的经验，即灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出一个持续学习框架，采用生成式经验回放模型，使可通行性预测能够随着新地形进行增量适应；该框架不存储过去的原始数据即可保留先前经验，并将回放模型生成样本的不确定性纳入考虑，实现不确定性感知的适应。

Result: 使用滑移转向机器人的真实世界实验验证了所提框架的有效性，表明其能够在多种不同环境间连续适应，同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 该框架为可通行性预测中的持续学习提供了有效方案，兼顾历史经验保留与不确定性感知的新地形适应，并在真实机器人平台上得到验证。

Abstract: Traversability prediction is a critical component of autonomous navigation in unstructured environments, where complex and uncertain robot-terrain interactions pose significant challenges such as traction loss and dynamic instability. Despite recent progress in learning-based traversability prediction, these methods often fail to adapt to novel terrains. Even when adaptation is achieved, retaining experience from previously trained environments remains a challenge, a problem known as catastrophic forgetting. To address this challenge, we propose a continual learning framework for traversability prediction that incrementally adapts to new terrains using a generative experience recall model. A key virtue of the proposed framework is two folds: i) retain prior experience without storing past data; and ii) incorporate the uncertainty of the generated samples from the recall model, enabling uncertainty-aware adaptation. Real-world experiments with a skid-steering robot validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, demonstrating its ability to adapt across a series of diverse environments while mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [308] [LiLi: Lie Theory Based 3D LiDAR Scan Alignment Degeneracy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17145)
*Vsevolod Hulchuk,Jan Bayer,Jan Faigl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LiLi方法，利用Lie理论识别SE(3)中的退化变换集合，实现稳健的3D LiDAR退化场景配准与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直走廊、平坦田野等退化场景中，3D LiDAR扫描配准解不唯一，影响定位与建图精度。现有退化检测方法忽略数据点重关联的可能性，对噪声和复杂退化敏感。

Method: LiLi基于Lie理论，在SE(3)刚性变换Lie群中识别完整退化变换集合；通过对优化解施加扰动并比较优化后的位姿，实现稳健退化检测；利用se(3)Lie代数生成元系统描述退化变换集合。

Result: 合成数据上较最先进的Hessian方法误差降低50%，在含噪声数据集上提升更显著；真实退化数据集上集成到LiDAR里程计，在260 m轨迹上定位优于Hessian参考方案，并在430 m往返隧道轨迹上成功，而参考方法失败。

Conclusion: LiLi通过Lie群/代数视角系统刻画退化变换，显著提升退化场景下LiDAR配准与定位的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we study 3D LiDAR scan alignment in challenging scenarios with degeneracies, such as straight corridors or flat fields, where the alignment solution is not unique and compromises localization and mapping accuracy. Existing degeneracy detection methods that neglect the potential for reassociating data points are prone to being sensitive to noise and complex degeneracies. Therefore, we propose LiLi - a novel method that leverages Lie theory to identify the full set of degenerate transformations within the SE(3) Lie group of rigid transformations. The method employs perturbations of the optimized solution and compares the resulting optimized poses to ensure robust detection of degeneracies. By leveraging generators from the Lie algebra se(3), the method provides a systematic approach to describing the set of degenerate transformations. Quantitative evaluations on synthetic data show significant improvement over the state-of-the-art Hessian-based method, reducing alignment error by 50%, with more significant improvements for datasets featuring noise. In the real-world degenerate datasets, the proposed method integrated into LiDAR-based odometry yields superior localization performance compared to the reference solution based on the Hessian-based degeneracy detector on a 260 m long trajectory, and succeeds on a 430 m long round-trip tunnel trajectory where the reference fails.

</details>


### [309] [Kernel-Based Metrics Learning for Uncertain Opponent Vehicle Trajectory Prediction in Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17147)
*Hojin Lee,Youngim Nam,Sanghun Lee,Cheolhyeon Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向自动驾驶赛车的异构核度量深度核学习方法，用于鲁棒预测轨迹不确定的对手车辆，并在1/10比例赛车平台上验证了其预测精度与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车中，对手车辆的驾驶策略未知、轨迹不确定，给安全超车带来重大挑战。现有方法难以在无监督条件下区分不同驾驶策略并给出可靠的轨迹预测及不确定性估计。

Method: 提出用于深度核学习（DKL）的异构核度量（heterogeneous kernel metrics），在无监督方式下，基于自车（EV）与对手车（OV）的交互观测，将相似驾驶策略对齐、将不同策略分离，从而进行精确轨迹预测并输出相关不确定性。

Result: 在1/10比例赛车平台上开展实验，证明所提方法提升了预测精度，并能安全超越对手车辆；同时方法在车载计算单元上计算高效，适合快速赛车环境。视频与源码见 https://github.com/HMCL-UNIST/OpponentPredictionWithKMDKL.git。

Conclusion: 异构核度量DKL能有效应对对手驾驶策略不确定性，实现精确且带不确定性的轨迹预测，兼顾安全性、计算效率与实车可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous racing confronts significant challenges in safely overtaking Opponent Vehicles (OVs) that exhibit uncertain trajectories, stemming from unknown driving policies. To address these challenges, this study proposes heterogeneous kernel metrics for Deep Kernel Learning (DKL), designed to robustly capture the diverse driving policies of OVs, and carry out precise trajectory predictions along with the associated uncertainties. A key virtue of the proposed kernel metrics lies in their ability to align similar driving policies and disjoin dissimilar ones in an unsupervised manner, given the observed interactions between the Ego Vehicle (EV) and OVs. The efficacy of the proposed method is substantiated through experimental studies on a 1/10th scale racecar platform, demonstrating improved prediction accuracy and thereby safely overtaking against OVs. Furthermore, our method is computationally efficient for onboard computing units, affirming its viability in fast-paced racing environments. The video and source code can be found at https://github.com/HMCL-UNIST/OpponentPredictionWithKMDKL.git.

</details>


### [310] [HuMemSLAM: Efficient Human-Inspired Semantic Place Recognition for Robust Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17168)
*Mayowa Adebambo,Sebastian Donnelly,Armand Amaritei,Andrew Bradley,Alexander Rast*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HuMem-VPR（受人类记忆与感知启发的视觉位置识别方法）及其与 ORB-SLAM3 的集成系统 HuMemSLAM，在保证高检索精度的同时大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统需要可靠的位置识别以支撑高效 SLAM。传统几何视觉 SLAM 依赖低层特征与几何一致性，易受感知歧义（不同地点相似）和感知变化（同一地点外观不同）影响。尽管语义 SLAM 与现代学习式 VPR 提升了鲁棒性，但实时部署仍要求高检索精度与低延迟兼得。

Method: 受人类记忆与感知机制启发，提出 HuMem-VPR，利用自下而上的感知证据与自上而下的上下文推理之间的双向关系，实现高层地点理解；并进一步将 HuMem-VPR 与 ORB-SLAM3 集成，构建 HuMemSLAM。

Result: HuMem-VPR 在真实图像基准上取得最高综合检索精度，在 CARLA 基准上具有竞争力，延迟比所评估的先进 VPR 方法低约 2–3 倍。在多个数据集族与在线实验中，HuMemSLAM 相比 ORB-SLAM3 原生检索显著提升了整体 Recall@1，同时减少了提交给几何后端的候选数量。

Conclusion: HuMem-VPR 兼顾高精度与低延迟，HuMemSLAM 通过集成该模块有效提升 SLAM 的位置识别性能并降低后端负担，验证了类人记忆与感知机制在实时 VPR 与 SLAM 中的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous systems require reliable place recognition for efficient and effective simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM). Traditional geometric visual SLAM approaches rely on low-level features and geometric consistency, but remain vulnerable to perceptual aliasing, where different places appear similar, and perceptual variation, where the same place appears different. Although semantic SLAM and modern learned visual place recognition (VPR) methods improve robustness under challenging perceptual conditions, real-time deployment requires both high retrieval accuracy and low latency. Inspired by human memory and perception, we propose HuMem-VPR, which exploits the bidirectional relationship between bottom-up perceptual evidence and top-down contextual reasoning to achieve high-level place understanding. We further introduce HuMemSLAM, the integration of HuMem-VPR with ORB-SLAM3. HuMem VPR achieved the highest aggregate retrieval accuracy on the real-image benchmark, competitive accuracy on the CARLA benchmark, and approximately two to three times lower latency than the evaluated state-of-the-art VPR methods. Across the evaluated dataset families and online experiments, HuMemSLAM substantially improved integrated Recall @1 over ORB-SLAM3's native retrieval while reducing the proposals submitted to its geometric backend.

</details>


### [311] [Fingers as Legs: Learning Self-Supported Locomotion and Manipulation with an Anthropomorphic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17172)
*Amirhossein Kazemipour,Hehui Zheng,Robert Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了一只拟人机械手如何在学习移动与操作技能的同时，保留其手指设计和位置控制器，实现无需独立移动机构的紧凑移动操作平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械手通常需要独立的移动机构，而本文探索让机械手直接用手指完成移动自身、支撑重量和与环境交互等任务，以构建自包含的紧凑移动操作平台。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，考虑机械手各手指的不对称性；在根据硬件测量校准的仿真器中进行训练，并设计了适合该平台的奖励函数；硬件上采用任务专用策略，同时使用机载电源与计算实现自包含。

Result: 仿真中，本文奖励公式使机械手移动速度优于为四足机器人设计的调优奖励；硬件上实现了无缆爬行、转向和跌倒恢复，并在支撑自身重量的同时执行连续键盘指令（无视觉）以及借助俯视视觉反馈将物体推向目标。

Conclusion: 结果表明可以构建一种紧凑的移动操作器，复用其手指进行移动和交互，而无需单独的移动机构。

Abstract: A walking robotic hand must use the same fingers to move its body, support its weight, and interact with the environment. We show how an anthropomorphic hand can learn these skills while retaining its finger design and position controller. Onboard power and computation make the platform self-contained. Our reinforcement learning approach accounts for the hand's unequal fingers, with training in a simulator calibrated from hardware measurements. In simulation, the hand moves faster with our reward formulation than with tuned rewards originally designed for quadrupeds. On hardware, task-specific policies enable untethered crawling, steering, and fall recovery. While supporting its own weight, the hand also executes successive keyboard commands without vision and pushes an object to targets using overhead visual feedback. These results demonstrate a compact mobile manipulator that reuses its fingers for locomotion and interaction, without a separate locomotion mechanism.

</details>


### [312] [Fleet-To-Lab: A Transfer Learning Framework For Lunar Rover Slippage Estimation Via Model Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17187)
*Riccardo Viviano,Saki Omi,Andrej Orsula,Miguel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fleet-to-Lab迁移学习框架，利用已部署异构月球巡视器的本体感知数据，通过新算法AcoMerge进行模型融合，缓解地月域差距，提升滑移估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的轮滑移估计对自主月球巡视器的移动与导航至关重要。基于地球数据训练的机器学习模型难以泛化到月球地形，而真实月球数据集因任务数量有限、采集成本高昂而稀缺，存在显著的地月域差距。

Method: Fleet-to-Lab迁移学习框架，利用已部署异构月球巡视器收集的本体感知数据，将其融合为单一架构，仅使用巡视器部署后采集的少量数据集。核心为AcoMerge——一种新的混合群体智能算法，通过搜索最优专家参数组合实现模型融合。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真实验中，相比深度模型融合基线，在平衡准确率和宏F1上均有提升。AcoMerge在深度架构联合训练上具竞争力，且在更小模型上实现更高的宏F1和平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 该框架表明模型融合可作为数据有限的空间机器人任务中滑移估计的一种可行迁移学习替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate wheel slip estimation is essential for autonomous lunar rover mobility and navigation. Machine Learning models trained on terrestrial data generalize poorly to lunar terrain, and real lunar datasets are scarce due to the limited number of missions and costly data acquisition. We present Fleet-to-Lab, a transfer learning framework that leverages proprioceptive data collected by previously deployed heterogeneous lunar rovers to mitigate the Earth-Moon domain gap in slip estimation for a future deployable unit. We fuse several heterogeneous expert models into a single architecture, using a modest dataset collected after the rover deployment. We propose AcoMerge, a new hybrid swarm-intelligence algorithm that performs model fusion by searching for an optimal combi- nation of expert parameters. Experiments conducted in a high- fidelity physics simulation show balanced accuracy and macro- F1 improvements compared to deep model fusion baselines. AcoMerge exhibits competitive performance with joint training on deep architectures, while achieving higher macro-F1 and balanced accuracy on a smaller model. Overall, our framework shows model fusion as a possible transfer learning alternative for slippage estimation in space robotic missions with limited data.

</details>


### [313] [TIO-Former: Ultra-Lightweight 6-Directional ToF-Inertial Odometry for Nano-UAVs via a Streaming Causal Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17198)
*Yang Liu,Yifan He,Wenhao Zhao,Xiangyu Mo,Yang Xu,Hao Wei,Mingze Ma,Huan Li,Yifan Wu,Zipeng Dai,Xin Zhou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TIO-FORMER，一种仅用IMU和6个正交8x8 ToF阵列（15g）的免视觉、免光流、无地图距离-惯性里程计框架，在纳米无人机上实现低漂移、有界计算与内存的6-DoF状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主纳米无人机导航受严苛的SWaP-C约束：视觉与LiDAR超重，光流在弱纹理场景退化，纯惯性估计易累积漂移；多区域ToF阵列虽轻量，但每帧仅384个距离测量，面临无效回波、各向异性可观性差和计算随时间增长等难题。

Method: 前端用双边门控差分配对相邻距离网格；IMU引导的交叉注意力根据平台运动学动态路由方向特征；采用流式因果Transformer，将未压缩的Local KV缓存与压缩Chunk-FIFO记忆结合，使推理成本与内存占用不随飞行时长增长。

Result: 真实飞行中开环位置误差比纳米无人机光流降低54.4%，比学习惯性基线降低66.4%–89.1%；在多种环境及严重传感退化下验证鲁棒性；部署于RISC-V边缘协处理器，P95延迟10.466ms，峰值常驻内存6.324MiB（不足系统RAM的5%）。

Conclusion: 稀疏距离感知可为资源受限的微型空中机器人提供实用的几何锚定，TIO-FORMER在极端SWaP-C条件下实现准确、稳健且计算有界的里程计。代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous nano-UAV navigation requires accurate ego-motion estimation under stringent size, weight, power, and computing (SWaP-C) constraints, where visual sensors and LiDARs exceed payload limits, optical flow degrades in low-texture scenes, and inertial-only state estimation is susceptible to accumulated drift. While multi-zone time-of-flight (ToF) arrays provide a lightweight metric complement, 6-DoF estimation from merely 384 ranges per frame is challenged by invalid returns, anisotropic observability, and temporal computational scaling. We propose TIO-FORMER, a camera-free, optical-flow-free, and mapless range-inertial odometry framework driven by an IMU and an ultra-lightweight (15 g) payload of six orthogonal 8 x 8 ToF arrays. Our frontend pairs consecutive range grids with a bilateral gated difference, while IMU-guided cross-attention dynamically routes directional features conditioned on platform kinematics. A Streaming Causal Transformer couples an uncompressed Local KV cache with compressed Chunk-FIFO memory, maintaining bounded inference cost and memory footprint independent of flight duration. In real-flight evaluations, TIO-FORMER reduces open-loop position error by 54.4% compared to nano-UAV optical flow and by 66.4%-89.1% over learned inertial baselines. We also evaluate performance across multiple environments and robustness under severe sensing degradation. Deployed on an edge RISC-V companion computer, TIO-FORMER achieves a P95 latency of 10.466 ms and peak resident memory of 6.324 MiB (less than 5 percent system RAM), demonstrating that sparse range sensing provides practical geometric anchoring for resource-constrained micro-aerial robots. Code is available at https://github.com/Ly041021/TIO-Former.

</details>


### [314] [FluxVLA Engine: A One-Stop VLA Engineering Platform for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17210)
*Yinhao Li,Weixin Mao,Zihan Lan,Jikun Rong,Qirui Hu,Yiming Zhang,Weipeng Deng,Bowen Shen,Minzhao Zhu,Yiming Mao,Yan Yang,Chenguang Cui,Hongyuan Chen,Xu Huang,Zheyi Zhao,Pinxi Shen,Bozhen He,Zhen Fu,Yifan Wang,Zexin Zhang,Ang Gao,Haoyu Chen,Chengqi Shi,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FluxVLA Engine是一个开源、配置驱动的平台，通过标准化异构具身策略组件接口，将数据到部署流程打通，解决VLA、世界动作模型与离线强化学习在工程落地上的碎片化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型、世界动作模型与离线强化学习方法快速发展，但数据格式、训练栈、评测协议、推理运行时和具身接口各不相同，导致算法难以转化为可靠的机器人系统。

Method: 不提出新策略模型，而是统一数据集、视觉语言与世界模型、动作头、奖励/优势加权学习、分布式训练、仿真评测、优化推理和机器人操作符等接口；集成双臂仿真、自动数据生成、人在回路rollout与纠正采集；结合实时分块(RTC)、加速推理后端、远程GPU服务及轨迹后处理。

Result: 通过共享且可审计的契约，将离线学习、仿真验证、在线纠正与真机执行串联起来，形成可复现的数据到部署工作流。

Conclusion: FluxVLA聚焦于弥合具身学习算法与可复现评测、可靠部署之间的工程瓶颈，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models, world-action models (WAMs), and offline reinforcement learning methods are rapidly expanding the design space of embodied policies, yet turning these algorithms into reliable robot systems remains constrained by fragmented data formats, training stacks, evaluation protocols, inference runtimes, and embodiment-specific interfaces. We present $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ Engine, an open, configuration-driven platform that turns heterogeneous embodied-policy components into a reproducible data-to-deployment workflow. Rather than introducing another policy model, $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ standardizes interfaces for datasets, visual-language and world models, action heads, reward- or advantage-weighted learning, distributed training, simulation evaluation, optimized inference, and robot operators. The engine further integrates compositional dual-arm simulation, scalable automatic data generation, and model-decoupled human-in-the-loop rollout, takeover, correction collection, and reward annotation. For responsive physical execution, it combines Real-Time Chunking (RTC) with accelerated inference backends, lightweight remote GPU serving, and configurable trajectory post-processing. Together, these capabilities connect offline learning, simulation validation, online correction, and real-robot execution through shared and auditable contracts. $\mathrm{FluxVLA}$ therefore targets the engineering bottlenecks separating promising embodied-learning algorithms from reproducible evaluation and dependable deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/FluxVLA/FluxVLA

</details>


### [315] [Swim-and-Breach at Palm Scale: A Rudder-Steered Two-Propeller Underwater Robot Platform with Differential-Thrust Pitch Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17240)
*Daehyun Choi,Ian Bergerson,Hengjia Zhu,Tianjun Lan,Saad Bhamla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种手掌大小(65 mm, 34 g)的游泳-跃出双模机器人平台，通过垂直叠放双螺旋桨实现推进与俯仰控制，尾舵控制偏航，实现游泳、跃出水面及无缝巡航-跃出-巡航序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建能跨越障碍和干涸间隙的小型机器人，用于溪流、被淹结构和工业系统的巡检，需要一种兼具高速游泳与跃出水面能力的紧凑平台。

Method: 采用两个垂直叠放螺旋桨提供推进并借助PID环路实现差动推力俯仰控制，尾舵增加偏航控制；船体经流场仿真优化，阻力较等效长方体降低五倍；螺旋桨使用B-series模型优化并以测力计测量验证。

Result: 机器人游泳速度达13.9体长/秒，转向速度209°/秒，处于水下机器人报道的上限水平；自由游泳时俯仰环可将机体转至任意指令抬头角度，尾舵稳定出水姿态，实现跃出高度1.6体长、距离3.7体长的无缝巡航-跃出-巡航序列。

Conclusion: 该平台可用于构建能跨越障碍和干涸间隙的小型机器人，适用于溪流、被淹结构和工业系统巡检。

Abstract: We present a palm-scale (65 mm, 34 g) swim-and-breach robot platform. Two vertically stacked propellers provide both propulsion and differential-thrust pitch control under a proportional-integral-derivative (PID) loop, and a tail rudder adds yaw control. The hull, evaluated by flow simulation, reduces the drag five-fold relative to an equivalent cuboid, and the propellers are optimized using B-series modeling validated by dynamometer measurements. The current robot swims at 13.9 body lengths per second and turns at 209 deg per second, corresponding to the upper limits reported for underwater robots. In free swimming, the pitch loop turns the body to any commanded nose-up pitch angle, and, with the rudder stabilizing the exit, the current robot leaps 1.6 body lengths high and 3.7 long in a seamless cruise-leap-cruise sequence. The platform can be used to build small-scale robots that cross barriers and dry gaps between pools for inspection in streams, flooded structures, and industrial systems.

</details>


### [316] [DriveMCP: An Agentic AI framework for Advanced Driver Assistance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17247)
*Farzad Nadiri,Mehdi Cina,Ahmad B. Rad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveMCP是一个模块化、可审计的智能体驾驶辅助框架，将感知、合规推理、车辆状态解释与安全仲裁整合为统一流水线，在CARLA仿真中降低了交通违规与超速并提升危险响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）直接驱动驾驶决策时，合规要素往往依赖仿真器注入的真值，缺乏可审计性与跨辖区合规推理能力，且难以融合车辆健康与天气等上下文，导致违规和超速风险较高。

Method: 提出DriveMCP架构：以类传感器感知栈配合DriveLM作为视觉语言前端，生成图结构场景理解与语言化驾驶信息；通过结构化解析层从DriveLM输出中推导限速与辖区线索等合规要素；由有状态编排层协调三个以MCP服务器形式暴露的专家（规则服务器做RAG合规推理、天气服务器估计牵引风险与速度建议、MCP-CAN服务器提供CAN/OBD遥测与诊断）；融合输出生成结构化决策，并由RSS启发的护栏在有界在线自适应下仲裁"播报"与"执行"。

Result: 在CARLA仿真中的多语言、跨境及动态限速场景下，DriveMCP相比VLM-Direct、VLM-Direct+RAG与VLM-Tools-NoArbiter基线减少了交通违规与超速，改善了危险响应时间，并保持亚秒级的建议延迟。

Conclusion: 模块化、可审计的智能体架构结合检索增强合规推理、多源上下文融合与RSS护栏仲裁，可有效提升驾驶辅助的安全合规表现，同时满足实时性要求。

Abstract: An agentic AI driver-assistance framework that integrates perception, compliance reasoning, vehicle-state interpretation, and safety arbitration into a modular and auditable pipeline. The architecture, referred to as DriveMCP, incorporates a sensor-like perception stack alongside DriveLM as the vision-language front end to generate a graph-structured scene understanding (Graph Visual Question Answering) and language-grounded driving information. Key compliance elements in world_state, including posted speed limits and jurisdiction cues, are derived from DriveLM outputs through a structured parsing layer rather than being injected as simulator ground truth. A stateful orchestration layer coordinates specialized experts exposed as Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers: (i) a Rules server that performs retrieval-augmented compliance reasoning over jurisdiction-specific traffic codes and sign conventions, (ii) a Weather server that estimates traction risk and contextual speed advisories, and (iii) an MCP-CAN server that surfaces Controller Area Network (CAN)/On-Board Diagnostics (OBD) telemetry and diagnostic context for health-aware risk shaping. These outputs are fused to generate a structured decision that prompts a recommended course of action. The outcome is then further filtered by a Responsibility-Sensitive Safety (RSS)-inspired guardrail that arbitrates speak versus act decisions under bounded online adaptation. In CARLA simulation across multilingual, cross-border, and dynamic speed-limit scenarios, DriveMCP reduces traffic infractions and overspeed relative to the VLM-Direct, VLM-Direct+RAG, and VLM-Tools-NoArbiter baselines, while improving hazard response time and maintaining sub-second advisory latency.

</details>


### [317] [Port-Hamiltonian Koopman Operator Synthesis for Mechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17249)
*Rajpal Singh,Aditya Singh,Jishnu Keshavan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保持结构（保能量/无源性）的Koopman框架，用于Euler-Lagrange系统：基于广义动量坐标将机械驱动暴露为已知的、状态无关的端口，并用结构约束神经网络联合学习提升函数与端口-Hamiltonian Koopman生成元，使学习到的动力学在构造上即是被动的，同时用Cayley-中点离散精确保持离散时间的储能-耗散平衡，从而避免递归预测中的能量增长与发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯从轨迹数据学习的有限维Koopman模型虽能实现高效线性预测与控制，但会破坏底层力学的能量结构，导致预测出现人为能量增长并在递归传播中发散。

Method: 1) 在广义动量坐标下构建保持结构的Koopman框架，将机械驱动表示为已知且状态无关的端口并在提升动力学中显式保留；2) 设计结构约束神经网络，联合学习提升函数与端口-Hamiltonian Koopman生成元，使所学动力学在构造上被动，而非依赖惩罚项或事后投影；3) 采用Cayley-中点离散，在离散时间精确保持储能-耗散平衡；4) 解析推导离散储能平衡及相应的稳定性保证。

Result: 仿真与实验表明，相比Koopman基线方法，所提方法在预测精度、数据效率和闭环跟踪方面均有提升，且对于更高维系统增益更大。

Conclusion: 通过结构与离散化层面的保结构设计，可在保证无源性与稳定性的同时提升Koopman模型对机器人等Euler-Lagrange系统的预测与控制表现。

Abstract: Finite-dimensional Koopman models enable efficient linear prediction and control of nonlinear robotic systems. However, models learned purely from trajectory data may violate the energetic structure of the underlying mechanics, producing predictions that exhibit artificial energy growth and diverge under recursive propagation. This work presents a structure-preserving Koopman framework for Euler-Lagrange systems built on generalized-momentum coordinates. The momentum transformation exposes the mechanical actuation as a known, state-independent port, which is preserved explicitly in the lifted dynamics. A structure-constrained neural architecture is developed to jointly learn the lifting functions and a port-Hamiltonian Koopman generator, rendering the learned dynamics passive by construction rather than through penalty terms or post-hoc projection. A Cayley-midpoint discretization further preserves the corresponding storage-dissipation balance exactly in discrete time. These properties are established analytically by deriving the discrete storage balance and associated stability guarantees of the learned predictor. Simulation and experimental studies demonstrate improved prediction accuracy, data efficiency, and closed-loop tracking over Koopman baselines, with increasing gains for higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [318] [CAD-Based Relation Learning and Geometric-Symbolic Planning for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17263)
*Fabian Harlacher,Christian Friedrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合学习式关系提取与几何符号推理的混合装配序列规划框架，可从非理想CAD数据生成可行的机器人拆解序列，成功率85.83%且规划时间大幅缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配序列规划（ASP）具有组合爆炸特性，穷举规划对复杂工业装配不实用；同时许多CAD模型缺乏可靠的语义接触信息或需大量人工预处理，限制了现有方法的适用性。

Method: 混合ASP框架：用神经网络从点云预测语义几何关系，并结合人在回路验证以修正不确定预测与规划失败；将提取的关系转化为符号装配图，由几何-符号规划器高效计算局部有效的机器人操作基元集合；采用基于可见性的射线投射策略引导搜索可行拆解方向，避免穷举组合搜索，并利用局部解空间进行序列优化。

Result: 在所引入的装配数据集和ASAP测试数据集上评估。ASAP数据集上规划成功率达85.83%，中位规划时间在所有装配规模上降低一个数量级以上，对超过30个零件的装配降低超过50倍（相对基线）。

Conclusion: 该混合框架能在非理想CAD数据下高效完成机器人装配序列规划，显著缩短规划时间；通过学习式特征分割、人在回路验证与几何-符号推理的结合，为可扩展、可适应的机器人装配与拆解规划提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Assembly Sequence Planning (ASP) remains a challenging problem due to its combinatorial nature, making exhaustive planning approaches impractical for complex industrial assemblies. Furthermore, many CAD models lack reliable semantic contact information or require extensive manual preprocessing, limiting the applicability of existing methods. This paper presents a hybrid ASP framework combining learning-based relation extraction with geometric-symbolic reasoning to generate feasible robotic disassembly sequences from imperfect CAD data. A neural network predicts semantic geometric relations from point clouds, while human-in-the-loop verification enables correction of uncertain predictions and planning failures. Extracted relations are transformed into a symbolic assembly graph, enabling a geometric-symbolic planner to efficiently compute locally valid sets of robotic manipulation primitives. A visibility-based ray-casting strategy guides the search for feasible disassembly directions without requiring an exhaustive combinatorial search, while the local solution space enables efficient sequence optimization. The framework is evaluated on an introduced assembly dataset and on the ASAP test dataset.
